const RELATIONAL_OPS = new Set(['==', '!=', '>', '<', '>=', '<=']);
const MESH_SHAPES = new Set(['lathe', 'torus', 'extrude', 'prism']);

function normalizeHeadOpcode(
  head: string,
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[],
): string {
  const upper = head.toUpperCase();
  if (upper === 'LLAMAR') return 'CALL';
  if (upper === 'BIEN' || upper === 'RETORNO') return 'RET';
  if (upper === 'FORMACION' || upper === 'FORMACIÓN') return 'ARRAY';

  const vocabHit = vocabulary.find((v) => v.glyphs === head || v.opcode.toUpperCase() === upper);
  if (vocabHit) return vocabHit.opcode.toUpperCase();

  if (head === 'ᛇᚢ') return 'KERN_BOOT';
  if (head === 'ᛗᚦ') return 'AUTH_CHECK';
  if (head === 'ᚠᚷ') return 'MEM_ALLOC';
  if (head === 'ᚠᚢ') return 'MEM_INIT';
  if (head === 'ᚠᛠ') return 'MEM_FREE';
  if (head === 'ᛒᚢ') return 'PROC_FORK';
  if (head === 'ᚱᚳ') return 'PROC_RUN';
  if (head === 'ᚳᛏ') return 'EXIT_OK';
  if (head === 'ᛣᚪ') return 'REG_PERSIST';
  if (head === 'ᚩᛖ') return 'IO_BUS_TX';
  if (head === 'ᛟᚪ') return 'FILE_WRITE';
  if (head === 'ᛟᚩ') return 'FILE_READ';
  if (head === 'ᛟᛤ') return 'FILE_DEL';
  if (head === 'ᚠᛗ') return 'SOA_INIT';
  if (head === 'ᛟᚱ') return 'CURL_STEP';
  if (head === 'ᛏᛜ') return 'NEMESIS_DECAY';
  if (head === 'ᛒᚣ') return 'BLOCH_ROT';
  if (head === 'ᛉᛣ') return 'BVN_LATTICE';
  if (head === 'ᚹᛋ') return 'WEBER_GUARD';
  return upper;
}

function isPlainIdentifier(tok: string): boolean {
  if (!tok || tok === AUTOMATON_BOS || tok === AUTOMATON_EOL || tok === AUTOMATON_EOS) {
    return false;
  }
  if (RESERVED_HEAD_OPCODES.has(tok.toUpperCase())) return false;
  if (RELATIONAL_OPS.has(tok)) return false;
  if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok)) return false;
  return /^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_.\-]*$/.test(tok);
}

function computeNextPhase(
  currentLineTokens: string[],
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[],
  declaredFunctions: Record<string, { params: string[] }>,
): GrammarPhase {
  if (currentLineTokens.length === 0) return 'LINE_HEAD';
  const headOp = normalizeHeadOpcode(currentLineTokens[0], vocabulary);
  const argCount = currentLineTokens.length - 1;

  switch (headOp) {
    case 'KERN_BOOT':
    case 'EXIT_OK':
    case 'END':
    case 'ELSE':
    case 'PROC_RUN':
    case 'REG_PERSIST':
    case 'MEM_LOCK':
    case 'MEM_FREE':
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'AUTH_CHECK':
    case 'PROC_FORK':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_IDENTIFIER' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'LOOP':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_LOOP_COUNT' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'MEM_ALLOC':
    case 'MEM_INIT':
    case 'HEAP_CAP':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_MEM_SIZE' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'SET':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'ADD':
    case 'SUB':
    case 'MUL':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_DECLARED_VAR';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'DIV':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_DECLARED_VAR';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_DENOM_P33';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'IF':
    case 'REQUIRES':
    case 'ENSURES':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_DECLARED_VAR';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_CMP_OP';
      if (argCount === 2) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'FILE_WRITE':
    case 'WS_EMIT':
    case 'IO_BUS_TX':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'FILE_READ':
    case 'FILE_DEL':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_WRITTEN_FILE' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'ARRAY':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_ARRAY_SIZE';
      if (argCount === 2) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'ASET':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_ARRAY_NAME';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_ARRAY_INDEX';
      if (argCount === 2) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'AGET':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_ARRAY_NAME';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_ARRAY_INDEX';
      if (argCount === 2) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'FN':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      return argCount <= 2 ? 'ARG_IDENTIFIER' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'CALL': {
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_FN_NAME';
      const fnName = currentLineTokens[1];
      const expectedParams = declaredFunctions[fnName]?.params.length ?? 0;
      if (argCount - 1 < expectedParams) return 'ARG_CALL_ARG';
      if (currentLineTokens[currentLineTokens.length - 1] === '->') return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';
    }

    case 'RET':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'MESH_NODE':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_MESH_SHAPE';
      if (argCount === 2) return 'ARG_MESH_TRIS';
      if (argCount === 3) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'MAT_ORM':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_MESH_NODE_ID';
      if (argCount >= 1 && argCount <= 3) return 'ARG_ORM_UNIT';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'CAM_6DOF':
      if (argCount < 6) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'SOA_INIT':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_SOA_CELLS' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'CURL_STEP':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      if (argCount === 1) return 'ARG_NONNEG_DT';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'NEMESIS_DECAY':
      return argCount === 0 ? 'ARG_UNIT_OPEN' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'BLOCH_ROT':
      if (argCount < 4) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      if (argCount === 4) return 'ARG_NONNEG_DT';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'BVN_LATTICE':
      if (argCount < 3) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      if (argCount === 3) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    case 'WEBER_GUARD':
      if (argCount === 0) return 'ARG_UNIT_OPEN';
      if (argCount < 3) return 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR';
      if (argCount === 3) return 'ARG_IDENTIFIER';
      return 'LINE_END_ONLY';

    default:
      return argCount === 0 ? 'LINE_END_ONLY' : 'LINE_END_ONLY';
  }
}

/**
 * Construye el estado exacto del Autómata Gramatical + Solver SMT
 * dado el prefijo de líneas completas y los tokens parciales de la línea en curso.
 */
export function createAutomatonContext(
  completedLines: string[],
  currentLineTokens: string[] = [],
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
): AutomatonState {
  const cleanCompleted = completedLines.map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const compiled = compileHelixScript(cleanCompleted.join('\n'), vocabulary);

  // Cerrar bloques abiertos temporalmente para que solveHelixConstraints evalúe multiplicadores LOOP y presupuestos exactos
  let openCount = 0;
  for (const inst of compiled) {
    const op = inst.opcode.toUpperCase();
    if (op === 'IF' || op === 'LOOP' || op === 'FN') openCount++;
    else if (op === 'END') openCount = Math.max(0, openCount - 1);
  }
  const solverInput =
    openCount > 0
      ? compileHelixScript(
          [...cleanCompleted, ...Array.from({ length: openCount }, () => 'END')].join('\n'),
          vocabulary,
        )
      : compiled;
  const sym = solverInput.length > 0 ? solveHelixConstraints(solverInput) : undefined;

  const blockStack: AutomatonState['blockStack'] = [];
  const declaredFunctions: Record<string, { params: string[] }> = {};
  const declaredArrays: Record<string, number> = {};
  const declaredVars: Record<string, IntervalBound> = sym
    ? { ...sym.variableBounds }
    : {};
  const meshNodes: string[] = [];
  const writtenFiles: string[] = [];
  let kernelBooted = false;
  let authenticated = false;
  let soaInitialized = false;
  let liveBlocksCount = 0;
  let unlockedBlocksCount = 0;
  let activeFnParams: string[] = [];

  for (const inst of compiled) {
    const op = inst.opcode.toUpperCase();
    if (op === 'KERN_BOOT') kernelBooted = true;
    else if (op === 'AUTH_CHECK') authenticated = true;
    else if (op === 'SOA_INIT') {
      soaInitialized = true;
      liveBlocksCount++;
      unlockedBlocksCount++;
    } else if (op === 'MEM_ALLOC' || op === 'MEM_INIT') {
      liveBlocksCount++;
      unlockedBlocksCount++;
    } else if (op === 'MEM_LOCK') {
      if (unlockedBlocksCount > 0) unlockedBlocksCount--;
    } else if (op === 'MEM_FREE') {
      if (unlockedBlocksCount > 0) unlockedBlocksCount--;
      if (liveBlocksCount > 0) liveBlocksCount--;
    } else if (op === 'FILE_WRITE' && inst.args[0]) {
      if (!writtenFiles.includes(inst.args[0])) writtenFiles.push(inst.args[0]);
    } else if (op === 'FILE_DEL' && inst.args[0]) {
      const idx = writtenFiles.indexOf(inst.args[0]);
      if (idx >= 0) writtenFiles.splice(idx, 1);
    } else if (op === 'IF') blockStack.push({ kind: 'IF', loopCount: 1 });
    else if (op === 'ELSE') {
      const top = blockStack[blockStack.length - 1];
      if (top?.kind === 'IF') top.kind = 'ELSE';
    } else if (op === 'LOOP') {
      const n = Number(inst.args[0]);
      blockStack.push({ kind: 'LOOP', loopCount: Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? Math.floor(n) : 1 });
    } else if (op === 'FN') {
      if (inst.args[0]) {
        const params = inst.args.slice(1);
        declaredFunctions[inst.args[0]] = { params };
        activeFnParams = params;
      }
      blockStack.push({ kind: 'FN', loopCount: 1 });
    } else if (op === 'END') {
      const popped = blockStack.pop();
      if (popped?.kind === 'FN') activeFnParams = [];
    } else if (op === 'SET' && inst.args[0] && !declaredVars[inst.args[0]]) {
      const v = Number(inst.args[1]);
      declaredVars[inst.args[0]] = Number.isNaN(v) ? { min: -Infinity, max: Infinity } : { min: v, max: v };
    } else if (op === 'CALL') {
      const arrowIdx = inst.args.indexOf('->');
      if (arrowIdx >= 0 && inst.args[arrowIdx + 1] && !declaredVars[inst.args[arrowIdx + 1]]) {
        declaredVars[inst.args[arrowIdx + 1]] = { min: -Infinity, max: Infinity };
      }
    } else if (op === 'ARRAY' && inst.args[0]) {
      const sz = Math.floor(Number(inst.args[1]));
      if (Number.isFinite(sz) && sz >= 1 && sz <= 256) {
        declaredArrays[inst.args[0]] = sz;
      }
    } else if (op === 'AGET' && inst.args[2] && !declaredVars[inst.args[2]]) {
      declaredVars[inst.args[2]] = { min: -Infinity, max: Infinity };
    } else if ((op === 'BVN_LATTICE' || op === 'WEBER_GUARD') && inst.args[3] && !declaredVars[inst.args[3]]) {
      declaredVars[inst.args[3]] = { min: 0, max: 1 };
    } else if (op === 'MESH_NODE' && inst.args[0] && !meshNodes.includes(inst.args[0])) {
      meshNodes.push(inst.args[0]);
    }
  }

  const insideFn = blockStack.some((b) => b.kind === 'FN');
  if (insideFn) {
    for (const p of activeFnParams) {
      if (!declaredVars[p]) {
        declaredVars[p] = { min: -Infinity, max: Infinity };
      }
    }
  }
  const loopMultiplier = blockStack.reduce(
    (acc, b) => (b.kind === 'LOOP' ? acc * b.loopCount : acc),
    1,
  );
  const lastInst = compiled[compiled.length - 1];
  const lastLineHead = lastInst ? lastInst.opcode.toUpperCase() : '';

  const phase = computeNextPhase(currentLineTokens, vocabulary, declaredFunctions);

  return {
    completedLines: cleanCompleted,
    currentLineTokens: [...currentLineTokens],
    phase,
    blockStack,
    loopMultiplier,
    insideFn,
    kernelBooted,
    authenticated,
    soaInitialized,
    liveBlocksCount,
    unlockedBlocksCount,
    writtenFiles,
    declaredVars,
    declaredArrays,
    declaredFunctions,
    meshNodes,
    remainingHeapBytes: Math.max(0, 1024 - (sym?.maxHeapBytes ?? 0)),
    remainingTriangles: Math.max(0, 10000 - (sym?.totalTriangles ?? 0)),
    lastLineHead,
  };
}

/**
 * Evalúa un único token candidato `tok` contra el estado actual del Autómata Gramatical
 * (CFG/PDA) + Base de 33 tokens + Invariantes SMT.
 */
export function evaluateTokenAgainstAutomaton(
  state: AutomatonState,
  tok: string,
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
  cells: HelixCell[] = [],
): TokenMaskDecision {
  if (tok === AUTOMATON_BOS) {
    return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_BOS', reason: 'Token <BOS> prohibido tras el inicio.' };
  }

  // Fase 1: Inicio de línea -> debe ser una cabecera válida en los 33 tokens o <EOS> al cerrar
  if (state.phase === 'LINE_HEAD') {
    if (tok === AUTOMATON_EOS) {
      const canClose =
        state.blockStack.length === 0 &&
        state.completedLines.length > 0 &&
        state.lastLineHead === 'EXIT_OK';
      return canClose
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : {
            token: tok,
            allowed: false,
            rule: state.blockStack.length > 0 ? 'UNCLOSED_BLOCK' : 'CFG_NEEDS_EXIT_OK',
            reason:
              state.blockStack.length > 0
                ? 'Prohibido <EOS> con bloques LOOP/IF/FN abiertos.'
                : 'El programa debe cerrar con ᚳᛏ (EXIT_OK) antes de <EOS>.',
          };
    }

    if (tok === AUTOMATON_EOL) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_EMPTY_LINE', reason: 'Se esperaba instrucción en LINE_HEAD.' };
    }

    const basisCheck = resolveInstructionToBasisRunes(tok, vocabulary, cells);
    if (!basisCheck.resolved) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'GODEL_OUT_OF_BASIS',
        reason: basisCheck.reason ?? `Token "${tok}" fuera de los 33 tokens Helix.`,
      };
    }

    const normOp = normalizeHeadOpcode(tok, vocabulary);
    if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok) || RELATIONAL_OPS.has(tok)) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_INVALID_HEAD', reason: 'Literal o símbolo no válido como opcode.' };
    }

    const needsBoot = new Set([
      'MEM_INIT',
      'MEM_ALLOC',
      'MEM_LOCK',
      'MEM_FREE',
      'HEAP_CAP',
      'FILE_WRITE',
      'FILE_READ',
      'FILE_DEL',
      'PROC_FORK',
      'PROC_RUN',
      'MESH_NODE',
      'MAT_ORM',
      'CAM_6DOF',
      'WS_EMIT',
      'ARRAY',
      'ASET',
      'AGET',
      'REG_PERSIST',
      'IO_BUS_TX',
      'SOA_INIT',
      'CURL_STEP',
      'NEMESIS_DECAY',
      'BLOCH_ROT',
      'BVN_LATTICE',
      'WEBER_GUARD',
    ]);
    if (needsBoot.has(normOp) && !state.kernelBooted) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'BOOT_PRECONDITION',
        reason: `${normOp} bloqueado antes de KERN_BOOT (ᛇᚢ).`,
      };
    }

    if ((normOp === 'FILE_WRITE' || normOp === 'FILE_DEL') && !state.authenticated) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'ACL_PRECONDITION',
        reason: `${normOp} bloqueado antes de AUTH_CHECK (ᛗᚦ).`,
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'END' && state.blockStack.length === 0) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'AST_ORPHAN_END', reason: 'END sin bloque abierto.' };
    }

    if (normOp === 'ELSE') {
      const top = state.blockStack[state.blockStack.length - 1];
      if (!top || top.kind !== 'IF') {
        return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'AST_ORPHAN_ELSE', reason: 'ELSE sin IF abierto.' };
      }
    }

    if (normOp === 'RET' && !state.insideFn) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'AST_ORPHAN_RET', reason: 'RET fuera de FN ... END.' };
    }

    if (normOp === 'EXIT_OK' && state.blockStack.length > 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'UNCLOSED_BLOCK',
        reason: `EXIT_OK prohibido con ${state.blockStack.length} bloque(s) abiertos.`,
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'FN' && state.insideFn) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'AST_NESTED_FN', reason: 'FN anidado prohibido.' };
    }

    if ((normOp === 'REQUIRES' || normOp === 'ENSURES') && !state.insideFn) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_CONTRACT_OUTSIDE_FN', reason: `${normOp} solo dentro de FN.` };
    }

    const varMutOps = new Set(['ADD', 'SUB', 'MUL', 'DIV']);
    if (varMutOps.has(normOp) && Object.keys(state.declaredVars).length === 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'TYPESTATE_UNINIT_VAR',
        reason: `${normOp} bloqueado: ninguna variable declarada con SET aún.`,
      };
    }

    if ((normOp === 'ASET' || normOp === 'AGET') && Object.keys(state.declaredArrays).length === 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'SMT_ARRAY_BOUNDS',
        reason: `${normOp} bloqueado: ningún arreglo ARRAY declarado aún.`,
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'CALL' && Object.keys(state.declaredFunctions).length === 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'AST_UNKNOWN_FN',
        reason: 'CALL bloqueado: ninguna función FN declarada aún.',
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'MAT_ORM' && state.meshNodes.length === 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'SCENE_NO_MESH',
        reason: 'MAT_ORM bloqueado: no hay MESH_NODE declarado.',
      };
    }

    if ((normOp === 'MEM_ALLOC' || normOp === 'MEM_INIT') && state.remainingHeapBytes <= 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'SMT_MEM_BOUNDS',
        reason: 'Heap agotado (0 B disponibles).',
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'SOA_INIT' && state.remainingHeapBytes < 28) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'SMT_MEM_BOUNDS',
        reason: 'Heap insuficiente (< 28 B) para celda Pauli-SoA.',
      };
    }

    if ((normOp === 'CURL_STEP' || normOp === 'NEMESIS_DECAY') && !state.soaInitialized) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'TYPESTATE_UNINIT_VAR',
        reason: `${normOp} bloqueado antes de SOA_INIT (ᚠᛗ).`,
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'MEM_FREE' && (state.unlockedBlocksCount < state.loopMultiplier || state.liveBlocksCount === 0)) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'TYPESTATE_DOUBLE_FREE',
        reason: 'MEM_FREE bloqueado: sin bloque desbloqueado disponible.',
      };
    }

    if (normOp === 'MEM_LOCK' && state.unlockedBlocksCount === 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'TYPESTATE_LOCKED_FREE',
        reason: 'MEM_LOCK bloqueado: sin bloque RW desbloqueado disponible.',
      };
    }

    if ((normOp === 'FILE_READ' || normOp === 'FILE_DEL') && state.writtenFiles.length === 0) {
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'VFS_ENOENT',
        reason: `${normOp} bloqueado: aún no existe ningún archivo escrito en el VFS.`,
      };
    }

    return { token: tok, allowed: true };
  }

  // Fase 2: Cierre obligatorio de línea (o '->' opcional tras argumentos posicionales de CALL)
  if (state.phase === 'LINE_END_ONLY') {
    const headOp = normalizeHeadOpcode(state.currentLineTokens[0] ?? '', vocabulary);
    if (
      tok === '->' &&
      headOp === 'CALL' &&
      !state.currentLineTokens.includes('->') &&
      state.currentLineTokens.length >= 2
    ) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: true };
    }
    return tok === AUTOMATON_EOL
      ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
      : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_EXPECT_EOL', reason: 'Se esperaba salto de línea \\n.' };
  }

  if (tok === AUTOMATON_EOL || tok === AUTOMATON_EOS) {
    // RET sin argumento puede cerrar en EOL, FN con >= 1 nombre puede cerrar en EOL, ARRAY con >= 2 args
    const headOp = normalizeHeadOpcode(state.currentLineTokens[0] ?? '', vocabulary);
    if (
      tok === AUTOMATON_EOL &&
      ((headOp === 'RET') ||
        (headOp === 'ARRAY' && state.currentLineTokens.length >= 3) ||
        (headOp === 'FN' && state.currentLineTokens.length >= 2) ||
        (headOp === 'CURL_STEP' && state.currentLineTokens.length >= 2) ||
        (headOp === 'BLOCH_ROT' && state.currentLineTokens.length >= 5) ||
        (headOp === 'BVN_LATTICE' && state.currentLineTokens.length >= 4) ||
        (headOp === 'WEBER_GUARD' && state.currentLineTokens.length >= 4) ||
        (headOp === 'CALL' &&
          state.currentLineTokens.length >= 2 &&
          !state.currentLineTokens.includes('->') &&
          (state.declaredFunctions[state.currentLineTokens[1]]?.params.length ?? 0) <=
            state.currentLineTokens.length - 2))
    ) {
      return { token: tok, allowed: true };
    }
    return {
      token: tok,
      allowed: false,
      rule: 'CFG_INCOMPLETE_ARGS',
      reason: `Faltan operandos para fase ${state.phase}.`,
    };
  }

  // Validar por fase de argumento exacta
  switch (state.phase) {
    case 'ARG_IDENTIFIER':
      return isPlainIdentifier(tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_EXPECT_IDENT', reason: `Se esperaba identificador, recibido "${tok}".` };

    case 'ARG_DECLARED_VAR': {
      const headOp = normalizeHeadOpcode(state.currentLineTokens[0] ?? '', vocabulary);
      if (headOp === 'ENSURES' && tok === 'RET_VAL') {
        return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'TYPESTATE_UNINIT_VAR', reason: `"${tok}" no es una variable inicializada.` };
    }

    case 'ARG_VALUE_OR_VAR': {
      const headOp = normalizeHeadOpcode(state.currentLineTokens[0] ?? '', vocabulary);
      // Si es argumento 2 de ARRAY (opcional inicializador), puede ser EOL o número
      const isNum = /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok);
      const isDeclVar = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok);
      if (isNum || isDeclVar) {
        if (headOp === 'MESH_NODE' && state.currentLineTokens.length === 4) {
          const scaleVal = isNum ? Number(tok) : state.declaredVars[tok]?.max ?? 1;
          if (scaleVal <= 0 || scaleVal > 8) {
            return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_MESH_BUDGET', reason: 'Escala 3D fuera de (0..8].' };
          }
        }
        return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      if ((headOp === 'FILE_WRITE' || headOp === 'WS_EMIT' || headOp === 'IO_BUS_TX') && isPlainIdentifier(tok)) {
        return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'TYPESTATE_OR_LITERAL', reason: `Valor inválido "${tok}" en ${headOp}.` };
    }

    case 'ARG_DENOM_P33': {
      if (/^-?\d+$/.test(tok)) {
        const n = Number(tok);
        if (n === 0) {
          return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_DIV_ZERO', reason: 'Divisor 0 bloqueado token-a-token.' };
        }
        const chk = checkDenominatorInP33(BigInt(Math.abs(n)));
        if (!chk.inSubringP33) {
          return {
            token: tok,
            allowed: false,
            rule: 'P33_DENOMINATOR',
            reason: `Divisor ${tok} fuera del subanillo P33 (residuo ${chk.remainderFactor.toString()}).`,
          };
        }
        return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok)) {
        const b = state.declaredVars[tok];
        if (b.min <= 0 && b.max >= 0) {
          return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_DIV_ZERO', reason: `Variable ${tok} puede valer 0.` };
        }
        return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_DIV_INVALID', reason: `"${tok}" no es divisor P33 válido.` };
    }

    case 'ARG_LOOP_COUNT': {
      if (/^-?\d+$/.test(tok)) {
        const n = Number(tok);
        if (n < 1 || n > 100) {
          return {
            token: tok,
            allowed: false,
            rule: 'BOUNDED_LOOP',
            reason: `LOOP ${tok} fuera del límite determinista [1..100].`,
          };
        }
        return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'BOUNDED_LOOP', reason: 'LOOP requiere entero literal en [1..100].' };
    }

    case 'ARG_MEM_SIZE': {
      let maxBytes = NaN;
      let minBytes = NaN;
      if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok)) {
        maxBytes = Number(tok);
        minBytes = maxBytes;
      } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok)) {
        maxBytes = state.declaredVars[tok].max;
        minBytes = state.declaredVars[tok].min;
      } else {
        return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'TYPESTATE_UNINIT_VAR', reason: `"${tok}" no inicializado para MEM_ALLOC.` };
      }
      if (minBytes <= 0 || maxBytes * state.loopMultiplier > state.remainingHeapBytes) {
        return {
          token: tok,
          allowed: false,
          rule: 'SMT_MEM_BOUNDS',
          reason: `${tok} (${maxBytes}×${state.loopMultiplier}=${maxBytes * state.loopMultiplier} B) excede heap restante (${state.remainingHeapBytes} B).`,
        };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: true };
    }

    case 'ARG_ARRAY_NAME':
      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredArrays, tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_ARRAY_BOUNDS', reason: `Arreglo "${tok}" no declarado.` };

    case 'ARG_ARRAY_SIZE': {
      if (/^\d+$/.test(tok)) {
        const n = Number(tok);
        if (n >= 1 && n <= 256) return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'BOUNDED_ARRAY', reason: 'Tamaño ARRAY debe estar en [1..256].' };
    }

    case 'ARG_ARRAY_INDEX': {
      const arrName = state.currentLineTokens[1];
      const arrSize = state.declaredArrays[arrName] ?? 0;
      let idxMin = NaN;
      let idxMax = NaN;
      if (/^-?\d+$/.test(tok)) {
        idxMin = Number(tok);
        idxMax = idxMin;
      } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok)) {
        idxMin = state.declaredVars[tok].min;
        idxMax = state.declaredVars[tok].max;
      } else {
        return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_ARRAY_BOUNDS', reason: `Índice "${tok}" inválido o no declarado.` };
      }
      if (idxMin < 0 || idxMax >= arrSize) {
        return {
          token: tok,
          allowed: false,
          rule: 'SMT_ARRAY_BOUNDS',
          reason: `Índice "${tok}" ∈ [${idxMin}, ${idxMax}] fuera de [0, ${arrSize - 1}].`,
        };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: true };
    }

    case 'ARG_FN_NAME':
      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredFunctions, tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'AST_UNKNOWN_FN', reason: `Función "${tok}" no declarada.` };

    case 'ARG_CALL_ARG': {
      const isNum = /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok);
      const isDeclVar = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok);
      return isNum || isDeclVar
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'TYPESTATE_UNINIT_VAR', reason: `Argumento de CALL "${tok}" inválido.` };
    }

    case 'ARG_CMP_OP':
      return RELATIONAL_OPS.has(tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_EXPECT_CMP', reason: 'Se esperaba operador relacional (== != > < >= <=).' };

    case 'ARG_MESH_SHAPE':
      return MESH_SHAPES.has(tok.toLowerCase())
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_EXPECT_SHAPE', reason: 'Forma 3D debe ser lathe|torus|extrude|prism.' };

    case 'ARG_MESH_TRIS': {
      if (/^\d+$/.test(tok)) {
        const tris = Number(tok);
        if (tris > 0 && tris * state.loopMultiplier <= state.remainingTriangles) {
          return { token: tok, allowed: true };
        }
        return {
          token: tok,
          allowed: false,
          rule: 'SMT_MESH_BUDGET',
          reason: `${tris}×${state.loopMultiplier} Δ excede presupuesto restante (${state.remainingTriangles} Δ).`,
        };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_MESH_BUDGET', reason: 'Se esperaba entero positivo de triángulos.' };
    }

    case 'ARG_ORM_UNIT': {
      if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok)) {
        const v = Number(tok);
        if (v >= 0 && v <= 1) return { token: tok, allowed: true };
        return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_ORM_BOUNDS', reason: `Canal PBR ${tok} fuera de [0, 1].` };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_ORM_BOUNDS', reason: 'Canal PBR ORM requiere valor numérico en [0, 1].' };
    }

    case 'ARG_MESH_NODE_ID':
      return state.meshNodes.includes(tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SCENE_NO_MESH', reason: `Nodo 3D "${tok}" no declarado en escena.` };

    case 'ARG_WRITTEN_FILE':
      return state.writtenFiles.includes(tok)
        ? { token: tok, allowed: true }
        : { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'VFS_ENOENT', reason: `Archivo "${tok}" no existe aún en VFS.` };

    case 'ARG_SOA_CELLS': {
      if (/^\d+$/.test(tok)) {
        const cells = Number(tok);
        const bytes = cells * 28;
        if (cells >= 1 && cells <= 256 && bytes <= state.remainingHeapBytes) {
          return { token: tok, allowed: true };
        }
        return {
          token: tok,
          allowed: false,
          rule: 'SMT_MEM_BOUNDS',
          reason: `SOA_INIT ${cells} (${bytes} B) excede [1..256] o heap restante (${state.remainingHeapBytes} B).`,
        };
      }
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_MEM_BOUNDS', reason: 'SOA_INIT requiere entero de celdas en [1..256].' };
    }

    case 'ARG_UNIT_OPEN': {
      let minV = NaN;
      let maxV = NaN;
      if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok)) {
        minV = Number(tok);
        maxV = minV;
      } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok)) {
        minV = state.declaredVars[tok].min;
        maxV = state.declaredVars[tok].max;
      } else {
        return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_PHYSICS_STABILITY', reason: `"${tok}" no es factor físico válido en (0, 1).` };
      }
      if (minV > 0 && maxV < 1) return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'SMT_PHYSICS_STABILITY',
        reason: `Factor ${tok} ∈ [${minV}, ${maxV}] fuera de (0, 1) (viola disipación contráctil o Weber-Fechner).`,
      };
    }

    case 'ARG_NONNEG_DT': {
      let minV = NaN;
      let maxV = NaN;
      if (/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(tok)) {
        minV = Number(tok);
        maxV = minV;
      } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.declaredVars, tok)) {
        minV = state.declaredVars[tok].min;
        maxV = state.declaredVars[tok].max;
      } else {
        return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'SMT_PHYSICS_STABILITY', reason: `Paso temporal "${tok}" inválido.` };
      }
      if (minV > 0 && maxV <= 1) return { token: tok, allowed: true };
      return {
        token: tok,
        allowed: false,
        rule: 'SMT_PHYSICS_STABILITY',
        reason: `Paso dt=${tok} fuera de (0, 1] estable.`,
      };
    }

    default:
      return { token: tok, allowed: false, rule: 'CFG_UNKNOWN_STATE' };
  }
}

/**
 * Calcula el vector completo de máscara token-a-token para todo el vocabulario |V| del Mini-GPT.
 */
export function computeTokenMaskVector(
  state: AutomatonState,
  vocab: string[],
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
  cells: HelixCell[] = [],
): TokenMaskVectorResult {
  const decisions = vocab.map((tok) =>
    evaluateTokenAgainstAutomaton(state, tok, vocabulary, cells),
  );
  const allowedMask = decisions.map((d) => d.allowed);
  const allowedCount = allowedMask.filter(Boolean).length;
  return {
    state,
    allowedMask,
    decisions,
    allowedCount,
    maskedCount: vocab.length - allowedCount,
  };
}

/**
 * Avanza exactamente 1 token el estado del autómata gramatical (de forma incremental).
 */
export function advanceAutomatonToken(
  state: AutomatonState,
  chosenToken: string,
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
): AutomatonState {
  if (chosenToken === AUTOMATON_EOL) {
    const lineStr = state.currentLineTokens.join(' ').trim();
    const nextCompleted = lineStr ? [...state.completedLines, lineStr] : state.completedLines;
    return createAutomatonContext(nextCompleted, [], vocabulary);
  }
  const nextLineTokens = [...state.currentLineTokens, chosenToken];
  return createAutomatonContext(state.completedLines, nextLineTokens, vocabulary);
}


==================== ARCHIVO: C:\HELIX\repo_oficial\src\utils\helixModuleGFluidShaders.ts ====================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import {
  BiopixelPhysicsOverrides,
  OpcodeForceFieldProfile,
} from './helixBiopixelSurface';

export const HELIX_OPCODE_TEXELS = 32;
export const HELIX_FLUID_FBO_SIZE = 64;

/**
 * Pass 1 (GPGPU WebGL Ping-Pong FBO): Vertex shader del quad completo de simulación.
 */
export const HELIX_NS_KV_SIM_VERTEX_SHADER = `
varying vec2 vSimUv;
void main() {
  vSimUv = uv;
  gl_Position = vec4(position.xy, 0.0, 1.0);
}
`;

/**
 * Pass 1 (GPGPU WebGL Ping-Pong FBO): Fragment shader que integra directamente en GPU:
 *  - Ecuación de Navier-Stokes incompresible: du/dt = -(u·∇)u + ν∇²u + f_curl + f_opcodes
 *  - Ecuación constitutiva viscoelástica Kelvin-Voigt: dε/dt = (σ_opcodes + p_fluid - E·ε) / η
 *  - Disipación exponencial Ley Némesis de Cauchy hacia el reposo 0.0f
 *
 * Empaquetado RGBA [0..1] compatible 100% con UnsignedByteType (WebGL1/WebGL2/SwiftShader):
 *   R = u_x * 0.25 + 0.5
 *   G = u_y * 0.25 + 0.5
 *   B = ε   * 0.35 + 0.5   (deformación viscoelástica Kelvin-Voigt)
 *   A = ω   * 0.25 + 0.5   (vorticidad escalar ∇×u)
 */
export const HELIX_NS_KV_SIM_FRAGMENT_SHADER = `
precision highp float;

uniform sampler2D tPrevState;
uniform sampler2D tOpcodeField;
uniform vec2 uTexelSize;
uniform float uFluidTime;
uniform float uFluidDt;
uniform float uNsViscosity;
uniform float uNsCurlGain;
uniform float uKvElasticityE;
uniform float uKvDampingEta;
uniform float uNemesisGamma;
uniform float uOpcodeImpulse;
uniform float uOpcodeCount;

varying vec2 vSimUv;

vec4 unpackFluid(vec4 enc) {
  return vec4(
    (enc.r - 0.5) * 4.0,
    (enc.g - 0.5) * 4.0,
    (enc.b - 0.5) / 0.35,
    (enc.a - 0.5) * 4.0
  );
}

vec4 packFluid(vec2 vel, float strain, float vort) {
  return clamp(vec4(
    vel.x * 0.25 + 0.5,
    vel.y * 0.25 + 0.5,
    strain * 0.35 + 0.5,
    vort * 0.25 + 0.5
  ), 0.0, 1.0);
}

vec2 curlForce2D(vec2 uv, float t) {
  float e = 0.012;
  float pU = sin((uv.y + e) * 12.566 + t * 1.6) * cos(uv.x * 9.424 - t * 0.9);
  float pD = sin((uv.y - e) * 12.566 + t * 1.6) * cos(uv.x * 9.424 - t * 0.9);
  float pR = sin(uv.y * 12.566 + t * 1.6) * cos((uv.x + e) * 9.424 - t * 0.9);
  float pL = sin(uv.y * 12.566 + t * 1.6) * cos((uv.x - e) * 9.424 - t * 0.9);
  float dPsiDy = (pU - pD) / (2.0 * e);
  float dPsiDx = (pR - pL) / (2.0 * e);
  return vec2(dPsiDy, -dPsiDx) * 0.085;
}

void main() {
  float dt = clamp(uFluidDt, 0.001, 0.033);

  vec4 c = unpackFluid(texture2D(tPrevState, vSimUv));
  vec4 l = unpackFluid(texture2D(tPrevState, vec2(fract(vSimUv.x - uTexelSize.x), vSimUv.y)));
  vec4 r = unpackFluid(texture2D(tPrevState, vec2(fract(vSimUv.x + uTexelSize.x), vSimUv.y)));
  vec4 u = unpackFluid(texture2D(tPrevState, vec2(vSimUv.x, min(1.0, vSimUv.y + uTexelSize.y))));
  vec4 d = unpackFluid(texture2D(tPrevState, vec2(vSimUv.x, max(0.0, vSimUv.y - uTexelSize.y))));

  // Advección semi-Lagrangiana -(u·∇)u
  vec2 backUv = vec2(
    fract(vSimUv.x - c.x * dt * 0.16),
    clamp(vSimUv.y - c.y * dt * 0.16, 0.0, 1.0)
  );
  vec4 adv = unpackFluid(texture2D(tPrevState, backUv));

  // Difusión viscosa Laplaciana ν∇²u
  vec2 lapVel = (l.xy + r.xy + u.xy + d.xy - 4.0 * c.xy);

  // Acumular forzamiento de los opcodes activos codificados en tOpcodeField (32 texels)
  vec2 opcodeSwirlForce = vec2(0.0);
  float opcodeTargetStress = 0.0;
  for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
    if (float(i) >= uOpcodeCount) break;
    vec4 opData = texture2D(tOpcodeField, vec2((float(i) + 0.5) / 32.0, 0.5));
    vec2 opCenter = opData.rg;
    float opTargetStrain = (opData.b - 0.5) * 2.0;
    float opSignedWeight = (opData.a - 0.5) * 4.0;

    float du = abs(vSimUv.x - opCenter.x);
    du = min(du, 1.0 - du);
    float dv = vSimUv.y - opCenter.y;
    float distSq = du * du * 14.0 + dv * dv * 9.0;
    float kernel = exp(-distSq * 3.8);

    float wave = 0.6 + 0.4 * sin(uFluidTime * (2.2 + float(i) * 0.35));
    vec2 tangent = normalize(vec2(-dv, du + 0.001));
    opcodeSwirlForce += tangent * opSignedWeight * kernel * wave;
    opcodeTargetStress += opTargetStrain * abs(opSignedWeight) * kernel * wave;
  }

  vec2 curlF = curlForce2D(vSimUv, uFluidTime) * uNsCurlGain;
  vec2 totalForce = (curlF + opcodeSwirlForce * 0.85 * uNsCurlGain) * (0.55 + uOpcodeImpulse);
  float viscDrag = exp(-uNsViscosity * 4.5 * dt);
  vec2 nextVel = clamp((adv.xy + totalForce * dt) * viscDrag + lapVel * min(0.22, uNsViscosity * dt * 6.0), vec2(-1.8), vec2(1.8));

  // Vorticidad escalar discreta ω = ∂v/∂x - ∂u/∂y
  float vort = clamp(0.5 * ((r.y - l.y) - (u.x - d.x)) * 2.5, -1.8, 1.8);

  // Acoplamiento Fluido-Estructura + ODE Viscoelástica Kelvin-Voigt:
  // σ_total = E·ε + η·dε/dt  =>  dε/dt = (σ_opcodes + p_fluido - E·ε) / η
  float fluidPressure = (r.x - l.x + u.y - d.y) * 3.2 + abs(vort) * 0.55;
  float sigmaDrive = (opcodeTargetStress * 18.0 + fluidPressure * 6.0) * (0.55 + uOpcodeImpulse);
  float strainRate = (sigmaDrive - uKvElasticityE * c.z) / max(0.8, uKvDampingEta);
  float nextStrain = (c.z + strainRate * dt) * (1.0 - clamp(uNemesisGamma, 0.001, 0.4) * dt * 2.2);
  nextStrain = clamp(nextStrain, -1.25, 1.25);

  gl_FragColor = packFluid(nextVel, nextStrain, vort);
}
`;

/**
 * Pass 2 (Vertex Shader de Deformación de Malla 3D):
 * Lee directamente tFluidState (resultado GPU de Navier-Stokes + Kelvin-Voigt) y
 * desplaza los vértices de la malla 3D por presión viscoelástica normal + flujo tangencial.
 */
export const HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_VERTEX_PARS = `
uniform sampler2D tFluidState;
uniform float uFluidTime;
uniform float uMeshDeformGain;
uniform float uHelixTension;
uniform float uHelixCurlGain;
uniform float uHelixCymaticHz;
uniform float uHelixQuantumPhase;
varying vec3 vHelixWorldPos;
varying vec3 vHelixWorldNormal;
varying float vHelixCurlEnergy;
varying float vHelixKvStrain;
varying float vHelixFluidVort;
`;

export const HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_VERTEX_CHUNK = `
#include <begin_vertex>
vec2 hUvSample = vec2(
  fract(atan(position.z, position.x) / 6.2831853 + 0.5),
  clamp(position.y * 0.45 + 0.25, 0.02, 0.98)
);
vec4 hFluidEnc = texture2D(tFluidState, hUvSample);
vec2 hFluidVel = (hFluidEnc.rg - 0.5) * 4.0;
float hKvStrain = (hFluidEnc.b - 0.5) / 0.35;
float hFluidVort = (hFluidEnc.a - 0.5) * 4.0;

float hFreq = clamp(uHelixCymaticHz / 220.0, 1.0, 4.0);
vec3 hCurl = vec3(
  cos(transformed.y * hFreq + uFluidTime * 2.4 + uHelixQuantumPhase) - sin(transformed.z * hFreq + uFluidTime * 1.9),
  cos(transformed.z * hFreq - uFluidTime * 2.1) - sin(transformed.x * hFreq + uFluidTime * 2.3),
  cos(transformed.x * hFreq + uFluidTime * 1.8) - sin(transformed.y * hFreq - uFluidTime * 2.6)
);
vec3 hTangent = normalize(cross(vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), objectNormal + vec3(0.0001, 0.0, 0.0)));
float hTensionScale = clamp(0.32 + uHelixTension * 0.72, 0.15, 1.65);

transformed += objectNormal * (hKvStrain * 0.085 * uMeshDeformGain * hTensionScale);
transformed += hTangent * (hFluidVel.x * 0.042 * uMeshDeformGain * hTensionScale);
transformed.y += hFluidVel.y * 0.032 * uMeshDeformGain * hTensionScale;
transformed += hCurl * (0.016 * clamp(uHelixCurlGain, 0.1, 1.8) * clamp(uHelixTension, 0.0, 1.5));

vHelixKvStrain = hKvStrain;
vHelixFluidVort = hFluidVort;
vHelixCurlEnergy = clamp(abs(hKvStrain) * 0.75 + abs(hFluidVort) * 0.45 + uHelixTension * 0.35, 0.0, 1.0);
vec4 hWorldPos = modelMatrix * vec4(transformed, 1.0);
vHelixWorldPos = hWorldPos.xyz;
vHelixWorldNormal = normalize(mat3(modelMatrix) * objectNormal);
`;

/**
 * Pass 2 (Fragment Shader Óptico-Reológico):
 * Combina dispersión cromática de Cauchy (700/546.1/435.8 nm), SSS, glasmorfismo Fresnel
 * y mapa térmico de vorticidad Navier-Stokes / tensión Kelvin-Voigt en el silicio GPU.
 */
export const HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_FRAGMENT_PARS = `
uniform float uHelixTension;
uniform float uHelixQuantumPhase;
uniform vec3 uHelixCauchyN;
uniform float uHelixSssStrength;
uniform float uHelixGlassStrength;
varying vec3 vHelixWorldPos;
varying vec3 vHelixWorldNormal;
varying float vHelixCurlEnergy;
varying float vHelixKvStrain;
varying float vHelixFluidVort;
`;

export const HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_FRAGMENT_CHUNK = `
vec3 hN = normalize(vHelixWorldNormal);
vec3 hV = normalize(cameraPosition - vHelixWorldPos);
float hNdotV = clamp(dot(hN, hV), 0.0, 1.0);
float hFresnel = pow(1.0 - hNdotV, 3.0);

vec3 hKeyDir = normalize(vec3(0.55, 0.80, 0.50));
float hWrap = clamp((dot(hN, hKeyDir) + 0.55) / 1.55, 0.0, 1.0);
float hBackScatter = pow(clamp(dot(hV, -hKeyDir) * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0), 2.5);
vec3 hSssTint = vec3(0.14, 0.62, 0.88) * hWrap + vec3(0.95, 0.45, 0.28) * hBackScatter * 0.45;

// Resalte reológico Kelvin-Voigt (expansión azul cian / compresión ámbar) + vorticidad NS
vec3 hKvTint = mix(
  vec3(0.96, 0.62, 0.16),
  vec3(0.18, 0.78, 0.98),
  smoothstep(-0.45, 0.45, vHelixKvStrain)
);
float hVortGlow = clamp(abs(vHelixFluidVort) * 0.42, 0.0, 0.55);

gl_FragColor.rgb += hSssTint * uHelixSssStrength * (0.14 + 0.24 * vHelixCurlEnergy);
gl_FragColor.rgb += hKvTint * uHelixSssStrength * hVortGlow * 0.26;

float hCauchySpreadR = clamp((uHelixCauchyN.x - 1.44) * 9.0, 0.05, 1.0);
float hCauchySpreadG = clamp((uHelixCauchyN.y - 1.44) * 9.0, 0.05, 1.0);
float hCauchySpreadB = clamp((uHelixCauchyN.z - 1.44) * 9.0, 0.05, 1.0);
vec3 hDispersion = vec3(hCauchySpreadR * 0.85, hCauchySpreadG * 0.98, hCauchySpreadB * 1.25);
float hPhaseMod = 0.85 + 0.15 * sin(uHelixQuantumPhase + hNdotV * 6.28318);
gl_FragColor.rgb += hDispersion * hFresnel * uHelixGlassStrength * hPhaseMod * (0.20 + 0.24 * clamp(uHelixTension, 0.0, 1.2));
#include <dithering_fragment>
`;

export interface ModuleGShaderUniformBag {
  tFluidState: { value: THREE.Texture };
  tOpcodeField: { value: THREE.DataTexture };
  uFluidTime: { value: number };
  uMeshDeformGain: { value: number };
  uNsViscosity: { value: number };
  uNsCurlGain: { value: number };
  uKvElasticityE: { value: number };
  uKvDampingEta: { value: number };
  uNemesisGamma: { value: number };
  uHelixTension: { value: number };
  uHelixCurlGain: { value: number };
  uHelixCymaticHz: { value: number };
  uHelixQuantumPhase: { value: number };
  uHelixCauchyN: { value: THREE.Vector3 };
  uHelixSssStrength: { value: number };
  uHelixGlassStrength: { value: number };
}

export interface ModuleGFluidEngineSnapshot {
  fboResolution: number;
  opcodeTexelsCount: number;
  activeInjectors: number;
  meshDeformGain: number;
  nsViscosity: number;
  nsCurlGain: number;
  kvElasticityE: number;
  kvDampingEta: number;
  nemesisGamma: number;
  gpuPassCount: number;
  opcodeDataHash: number;
}

/**
 * Empaqueta los opcodes compilados de Helix VM en una DataTexture RGBA (32×1)
 * para lectura directa O(1) en los shaders WebGL de Navier-Stokes y Kelvin-Voigt.
 */
export function encodeVmOpcodesToFluidDataTexture(
  profile: OpcodeForceFieldProfile,
  existingTexture?: THREE.DataTexture,
): { texture: THREE.DataTexture; hash: number; activeCount: number } {
  const data =
    existingTexture && existingTexture.image.data instanceof Uint8Array
      ? existingTexture.image.data
      : new Uint8Array(HELIX_OPCODE_TEXELS * 4);

  data.fill(128);
  const count = Math.min(HELIX_OPCODE_TEXELS, profile.injectors.length);
  let hash = 2166136261;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const inj = profile.injectors[i];
    const off = i * 4;
    const r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(inj.uNorm * 255)));
    const g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(inj.vNorm * 255)));
    const b = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(255, Math.round((Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, inj.targetStrain)) * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255)),
    );
    const signedW = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, inj.vorticitySign * (inj.weight / 1.5)));
    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round((signedW * 0.5 + 0.5) * 255)));

    data[off] = r;
    data[off + 1] = g;
    data[off + 2] = b;
    data[off + 3] = a;

    hash ^= (r << 16) ^ (g << 8) ^ b ^ a;
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619) >>> 0;
  }

  if (existingTexture) {
    existingTexture.needsUpdate = true;
    return { texture: existingTexture, hash, activeCount: count };
  }

  const texture = new THREE.DataTexture(
    data,
    HELIX_OPCODE_TEXELS,
    1,
    THREE.RGBAFormat,
    THREE.UnsignedByteType,
  );
  texture.colorSpace = THREE.NoColorSpace;
  texture.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
  texture.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter;
  texture.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  texture.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
  texture.needsUpdate = true;
  return { texture, hash, activeCount: count };
}

/**
 * Inyecta los shaders WebGL de deformación Navier-Stokes + Kelvin-Voigt + Óptica Cauchy/SSS
 * en cualquier MeshPhysicalMaterial / MeshStandardMaterial de Three.js.
 */
export function attachHelixFluidKelvinVoigtShader(
  material: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial | THREE.MeshStandardMaterial,
  uniforms: ModuleGShaderUniformBag,
  cacheTag = 'helix-module-g-ns-kv-v1',
): void {
  material.customProgramCacheKey = () => cacheTag;
  material.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.tFluidState = uniforms.tFluidState;
    shader.uniforms.tOpcodeField = uniforms.tOpcodeField;
    shader.uniforms.uFluidTime = uniforms.uFluidTime;
    shader.uniforms.uMeshDeformGain = uniforms.uMeshDeformGain;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixTension = uniforms.uHelixTension;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixCurlGain = uniforms.uHelixCurlGain;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixCymaticHz = uniforms.uHelixCymaticHz;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixQuantumPhase = uniforms.uHelixQuantumPhase;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixCauchyN = uniforms.uHelixCauchyN;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixSssStrength = uniforms.uHelixSssStrength;
    shader.uniforms.uHelixGlassStrength = uniforms.uHelixGlassStrength;

    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader
      .replace('#include <common>', `#include <common>\n${HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_VERTEX_PARS}`)
      .replace('#include <begin_vertex>', HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_VERTEX_CHUNK);

    shader.fragmentShader = shader.fragmentShader
      .replace('#include <common>', `#include <common>\n${HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_FRAGMENT_PARS}`)
      .replace('#include <dithering_fragment>', HELIX_MESH_KV_DEFORM_FRAGMENT_CHUNK);
  };
}

/**
 * Motor GPGPU Ping-Pong WebGL para Navier-Stokes + Kelvin-Voigt acoplado a opcodes de la VM.
 * Funciona de forma determinista y sin extensiones float obligatorias (100% compatible SwiftShader/WebGL2).
 */
export class HelixWebGLFluidEngine {
  readonly fboSize = HELIX_FLUID_FBO_SIZE;
  readonly opcodeTexture: THREE.DataTexture;
  readonly sharedUniforms: ModuleGShaderUniformBag;

  private rtA: THREE.WebGLRenderTarget;
  private rtB: THREE.WebGLRenderTarget;
  private ping = true;
  private simScene: THREE.Scene;
  private simCamera: THREE.OrthographicCamera;
  private simQuadGeo: THREE.PlaneGeometry;
  private simMaterial: THREE.ShaderMaterial;
  private simMesh: THREE.Mesh;

  private activeInjectors = 1;
  private opcodeDataHash = 0;
  private gpuPassCount = 0;
  private impulse = 0.65;

  constructor(initialProfile: OpcodeForceFieldProfile, initialCauchyN = new THREE.Vector3(1.465, 1.471, 1.48)) {
    const encoded = encodeVmOpcodesToFluidDataTexture(initialProfile);
    this.opcodeTexture = encoded.texture;
    this.activeInjectors = encoded.activeCount;
    this.opcodeDataHash = encoded.hash;

    const rtOpts: THREE.RenderTargetOptions = {
      type: THREE.UnsignedByteType,
      format: THREE.RGBAFormat,
      minFilter: THREE.LinearFilter,
      magFilter: THREE.LinearFilter,
      wrapS: THREE.RepeatWrapping,
      wrapT: THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping,
      depthBuffer: false,
      stencilBuffer: false,
    };

    this.rtA = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(this.fboSize, this.fboSize, rtOpts);
    this.rtB = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(this.fboSize, this.fboSize, rtOpts);

    this.sharedUniforms = {
      tFluidState: { value: this.rtA.texture },
      tOpcodeField: { value: this.opcodeTexture },
      uFluidTime: { value: 0 },
      uMeshDeformGain: { value: 1.0 },
      uNsViscosity: { value: initialProfile.kinematicViscosity },
      uNsCurlGain: { value: initialProfile.curlStrength },
      uKvElasticityE: { value: initialProfile.elasticityE },
      uKvDampingEta: { value: initialProfile.dampingEta },
      uNemesisGamma: { value: initialProfile.nemesisGamma },
      uHelixTension: { value: 0.5 },
      uHelixCurlGain: { value: initialProfile.curlStrength },
      uHelixCymaticHz: { value: initialProfile.dominantFrequencyHz },
      uHelixQuantumPhase: { value: 0.7854 },
      uHelixCauchyN: { value: initialCauchyN.clone() },
      uHelixSssStrength: { value: 0.65 },
      uHelixGlassStrength: { value: 0.6 },
    };

    this.simMaterial = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: {
        tPrevState: { value: this.rtA.texture },
        tOpcodeField: { value: this.opcodeTexture },
        uTexelSize: { value: new THREE.Vector2(1 / this.fboSize, 1 / this.fboSize) },
        uFluidTime: this.sharedUniforms.uFluidTime,
        uFluidDt: { value: 1 / 60 },
        uNsViscosity: this.sharedUniforms.uNsViscosity,
        uNsCurlGain: this.sharedUniforms.uNsCurlGain,
        uKvElasticityE: this.sharedUniforms.uKvElasticityE,
        uKvDampingEta: this.sharedUniforms.uKvDampingEta,
        uNemesisGamma: this.sharedUniforms.uNemesisGamma,
        uOpcodeImpulse: { value: this.impulse },
        uOpcodeCount: { value: this.activeInjectors },
      },
      vertexShader: HELIX_NS_KV_SIM_VERTEX_SHADER,
      fragmentShader: HELIX_NS_KV_SIM_FRAGMENT_SHADER,
      depthTest: false,
      depthWrite: false,
    });

    this.simQuadGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2);
    this.simMesh = new THREE.Mesh(this.simQuadGeo, this.simMaterial);
    this.simScene = new THREE.Scene();
    this.simScene.add(this.simMesh);
    this.simCamera = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-1, 1, 1, -1, 0, 1);
  }

  /**
   * Inicializa los Ping-Pong FBOs al estado de reposo neutro (0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5) = (u=0, ε=0, ω=0).
   */
  resetBuffers(renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer): void {
    const prevTarget = renderer.getRenderTarget();
    const prevClear = renderer.getClearColor(new THREE.Color());
    const prevAlpha = renderer.getClearAlpha();

    renderer.setClearColor(new THREE.Color(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), 0.5);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(this.rtA);
    renderer.clear(true, false, false);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(this.rtB);
    renderer.clear(true, false, false);

    renderer.setClearColor(prevClear, prevAlpha);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(prevTarget);
  }

  syncOpcodes(profile: OpcodeForceFieldProfile): ModuleGFluidEngineSnapshot {
    const encoded = encodeVmOpcodesToFluidDataTexture(profile, this.opcodeTexture);
    this.activeInjectors = encoded.activeCount;
    this.opcodeDataHash = encoded.hash;

    this.sharedUniforms.uNsViscosity.value = profile.kinematicViscosity;
    this.sharedUniforms.uNsCurlGain.value = profile.curlStrength;
    this.sharedUniforms.uKvElasticityE.value = profile.elasticityE;
    this.sharedUniforms.uKvDampingEta.value = profile.dampingEta;
    this.sharedUniforms.uNemesisGamma.value = profile.nemesisGamma;
    this.sharedUniforms.uHelixCurlGain.value = profile.curlStrength;
    this.sharedUniforms.uHelixCymaticHz.value = profile.dominantFrequencyHz;
    this.simMaterial.uniforms.uOpcodeCount.value = encoded.activeCount;
    this.impulse = Math.max(this.impulse, 0.85);

    return this.getSnapshot();
  }

  pulse(strength = 1.2): void {
    this.impulse = Math.max(this.impulse, Math.min(2.0, strength));
  }

  step(
    renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer,
    dtSec: number,
    overrides: BiopixelPhysicsOverrides = {},
    meshDeformGain = 1.0,
  ): void {
    const dt = Math.min(0.033, Math.max(0.001, dtSec));
    this.sharedUniforms.uFluidTime.value += dt;
    this.sharedUniforms.uMeshDeformGain.value = meshDeformGain;
    this.impulse = Math.max(0, this.impulse * Math.exp(-dt * 1.15));

    if (overrides.curlStrength !== undefined) {
      this.sharedUniforms.uNsCurlGain.value = overrides.curlStrength;
      this.sharedUniforms.uHelixCurlGain.value = overrides.curlStrength;
    }
    if (overrides.elasticityE !== undefined) {
      this.sharedUniforms.uKvElasticityE.value = overrides.elasticityE;
    }
    if (overrides.dampingEta !== undefined) {
      this.sharedUniforms.uKvDampingEta.value = overrides.dampingEta;
    }

    this.simMaterial.uniforms.uFluidDt.value = dt;
    this.simMaterial.uniforms.uOpcodeImpulse.value = this.impulse;

    const readRt = this.ping ? this.rtA : this.rtB;
    const writeRt = this.ping ? this.rtB : this.rtA;
    this.simMaterial.uniforms.tPrevState.value = readRt.texture;

    const prevTarget = renderer.getRenderTarget();
    renderer.setRenderTarget(writeRt);
    renderer.render(this.simScene, this.simCamera);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(prevTarget);

    this.sharedUniforms.tFluidState.value = writeRt.texture;
    this.ping = !this.ping;
    this.gpuPassCount += 1;
  }

  getSnapshot(): ModuleGFluidEngineSnapshot {
    return {
      fboResolution: this.fboSize,
      opcodeTexelsCount: HELIX_OPCODE_TEXELS,
      activeInjectors: this.activeInjectors,
      meshDeformGain: this.sharedUniforms.uMeshDeformGain.value,
      nsViscosity: this.sharedUniforms.uNsViscosity.value,
      nsCurlGain: this.sharedUniforms.uNsCurlGain.value,
      kvElasticityE: this.sharedUniforms.uKvElasticityE.value,
      kvDampingEta: this.sharedUniforms.uKvDampingEta.value,
      nemesisGamma: this.sharedUniforms.uNemesisGamma.value,
      gpuPassCount: this.gpuPassCount,
      opcodeDataHash: this.opcodeDataHash,
    };
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.rtA.dispose();
    this.rtB.dispose();
    this.opcodeTexture.dispose();
    this.simQuadGeo.dispose();
    this.simMaterial.dispose();
  }
}

const BOS_TOKEN = '<BOS>';
const EOL_TOKEN = '\n';
const EOS_TOKEN = '<EOS>';

// Semilla determinista (PRNG Mulberry32) para inicialización reproducible, dropout y muestreo multinomial
function createRng(seed: number) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a |= 0;
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

function initArray(len: number, scale: number, rng: () => number): Float32Array {
  const arr = new Float32Array(len);
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    arr[i] = (rng() * 2 - 1) * scale;
  }
  return arr;
}

function initOnes(len: number): Float32Array {
  const arr = new Float32Array(len);
  arr.fill(1);
  return arr;
}

/**
 * Tokenizador simbólico para programas Helix: preserva runas, opcodes,
 * números, identificadores y saltos de línea para que el Transformer aprenda
 * la estructura gramatical exacta instrucción por instrucción.
 */
export function tokenizeHelixLine(line: string): string[] {
  const clean = line.split('#')[0].trim();
  if (!clean) return [];
  return clean.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
}

export function buildHelixNeuralCorpus(
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
  extraScripts: string[] = [],
): { sequences: string[][]; corpusText: string } {
  const baseScripts = [
    DEFAULT_HELIX_PROGRAM,
    DEFAULT_HELIX_3D_PROGRAM,
    DEFAULT_HELIX_CONTRACT_PROGRAM,
    DEFAULT_HELIX_PHYSICS_PROGRAM,
    `ᛇᚢ
ᛗᚦ treasury
SET fondo 1260
DIV fondo 14
MUL fondo 2
FILE_WRITE ledger.p33 fondo
ᚳᛏ`,
    `ᛇᚢ
ᛗᚦ root
SET tam 128
LOOP 2
  ᚠᚷ tam
END
ᛒᚢ worker
ᚱᚳ
FILE_WRITE init.cfg boot_ok
ᚳᛏ`,
    `ᛇᚢ
ᛗᚦ telemetry
SET pkt 10
LOOP 3
  WS_EMIT pulse pkt
  ADD pkt 1
END
ᚳᛏ`,
    `ᛇᚢ
ᛗᚦ render_admin
MESH_NODE hub lathe 960 1.0
MAT_ORM hub 0.92 0.28 0.85
MESH_NODE ring torus 1280 1.1
CAM_6DOF 3.2 2.1 3.8 38 18 45
WS_EMIT telemetry rotor_ready
ᚳᛏ`,
    ...extraScripts,
  ];

  // También añadimos mini-patrones desde el vocabulario compuesto del usuario
  const customOpSnippets = vocabulary
    .filter((w) => ['SET', 'FILE_WRITE', 'AUTH_CHECK'].every((op) => w.opcode !== op))
    .slice(0, 6)
    .map(
      (w) => `ᛇᚢ
ᛗᚦ sys_admin
${w.glyphs}
ᚳᛏ`,
    );

  const allScripts = [...baseScripts, ...customOpSnippets];
  const sequences: string[][] = [];

  for (const script of allScripts) {
    const tokens: string[] = [BOS_TOKEN];
    const lines = script.split(/\r?\n/);
    for (const rawLine of lines) {
      const lineTokens = tokenizeHelixLine(rawLine);
      if (lineTokens.length === 0) continue;
      tokens.push(...lineTokens, EOL_TOKEN);
    }
    tokens.push(EOS_TOKEN);
    if (tokens.length >= 4) {
      sequences.push(tokens);
    }
  }

  return {
    sequences,
    corpusText: allScripts.join('\n\n'),
  };
}

function getWeightList(w: NeuralWeights): Float32Array[] {
  return [
    w.tokEmb,
    w.posEmb,
    w.ln1Gamma,
    w.ln1Beta,
    w.wq,
    w.wk,
    w.wv,
    w.wo,
    w.ln2Gamma,
    w.ln2Beta,
    w.w1,
    w.b1,
    w.w2,
    w.b2,
    w.lnFGamma,
    w.lnFBeta,
    w.wLm,
    w.bLm,
  ];
}

export function createHelixNeuralModel(
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
  extraScripts: string[] = [],
  config: NeuralModelConfig = DEFAULT_NEURAL_CONFIG,
  seed = 1337,
): { model: HelixNeuralModel; sequences: string[][] } {
  const { sequences } = buildHelixNeuralCorpus(vocabulary, extraScripts);
  const tokenSet = new Set<string>([BOS_TOKEN, EOL_TOKEN, EOS_TOKEN]);

  // Asegurar que opcodes y argumentos clave (incluyendo leyes físicas/cuánticas y distractores para contraste en máscara) estén en el vocabulario
  const essentialTokens = [
    'ᛇᚢ',
    'ᛗᚦ',
    'root',
    'SET',
    'ADD',
    'SUB',
    'MUL',
    'DIV',
    'tam',
    'fondo',
