    '14',
    '0',
    '139',
    'LOOP',
    '2',
    '3',
    '250',
    'ᚠᚷ',
    'END',
    'IF',
    'ELSE',
    '>',
    'ᛒᚢ',
    'worker',
    'ᚱᚳ',
    'FILE_WRITE',
    'init.cfg',
    'boot_ok',
    'MESH_NODE',
    'MAT_ORM',
    'CAM_6DOF',
    'WS_EMIT',
    'FN',
    'REQUIRES',
    'ENSURES',
    'CALL',
    'RET',
    'RET_VAL',
    '->',
    '>=',
    'ARRAY',
    'ASET',
    'AGET',
    'asignar_cuota',
    'cuotas',
    'cuota0',
    'base',
    'total',
    'leido0',
    '1',
    '4',
    '32',
    '96',
    'SOA_INIT',
    'CURL_STEP',
    'NEMESIS_DECAY',
    'BLOCH_ROT',
    'BVN_LATTICE',
    'WEBER_GUARD',
    '16',
    '0.45',
    '0.008',
    '0.08',
    '1.5708',
    '3.1416',
    '0.015',
    '1.0',
    '1.004',
    'q_nondist',
    'wf_render',
    'physics.hlx',
    'EXEC_BAD',
    'ᛊ',
    'ᚳᛏ',
  ];
  for (const t of essentialTokens) tokenSet.add(t);
  for (const seq of sequences) {
    for (const tok of seq) tokenSet.add(tok);
  }

  const vocab = Array.from(tokenSet);
  const stoi = new Map<string, number>();
  vocab.forEach((tok, idx) => stoi.set(tok, idx));

  const V = vocab.length;
  const D = config.dModel;
  const F = config.ffnDim;
  const B = config.blockSize;
  const rng = createRng(seed);

  const scaleD = Math.sqrt(1 / D) * 0.5;
  const scaleF = Math.sqrt(1 / F) * 0.5;
  const weights: NeuralWeights = {
    tokEmb: initArray(V * D, 0.04, rng),
    posEmb: initArray(B * D, 0.03, rng),
    ln1Gamma: initOnes(D),
    ln1Beta: new Float32Array(D),
    wq: initArray(D * D, scaleD, rng),
    wk: initArray(D * D, scaleD, rng),
    wv: initArray(D * D, scaleD, rng),
    wo: initArray(D * D, scaleD, rng),
    ln2Gamma: initOnes(D),
    ln2Beta: new Float32Array(D),
    w1: initArray(D * F, scaleD, rng),
    b1: new Float32Array(F),
    w2: initArray(F * D, scaleF, rng),
    b2: new Float32Array(D),
    lnFGamma: initOnes(D),
    lnFBeta: new Float32Array(D),
    wLm: initArray(D * V, scaleD, rng),
    bLm: new Float32Array(V),
  };

  const wList = getWeightList(weights);
  const parameterCount = wList.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.length, 0);
  const moments: AdamMoments = {
    m: wList.map((a) => new Float32Array(a.length)),
    v: wList.map((a) => new Float32Array(a.length)),
    t: 0,
  };

  return {
    model: {
      config,
      vocab,
      stoi,
      weights,
      moments,
      trainedSteps: 0,
      parameterCount,
      lossHistory: [],
      sequencesCount: sequences.length,
    },
    sequences,
  };
}

/**
 * Normalización Pre-LN completa (LayerNorm con media, varianza, gamma y beta).
 */
function layerNormForward(
  x: Float32Array,
  gamma: Float32Array,
  beta: Float32Array,
  T: number,
  D: number,
): { out: Float32Array; xHat: Float32Array; invStd: Float32Array } {
  const out = new Float32Array(T * D);
  const xHat = new Float32Array(T * D);
  const invStd = new Float32Array(T);
  const eps = 1e-5;
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const off = t * D;
    let sum = 0;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) sum += x[off + c];
    const mean = sum / D;
    let sumSq = 0;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      const diff = x[off + c] - mean;
      sumSq += diff * diff;
    }
    const inv = 1 / Math.sqrt(sumSq / D + eps);
    invStd[t] = inv;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      const xh = (x[off + c] - mean) * inv;
      xHat[off + c] = xh;
      out[off + c] = xh * gamma[c] + beta[c];
    }
  }
  return { out, xHat, invStd };
}

function layerNormBackward(
  dOut: Float32Array,
  xHat: Float32Array,
  gamma: Float32Array,
  invStd: Float32Array,
  dX: Float32Array,
  dGamma: Float32Array,
  dBeta: Float32Array,
  T: number,
  D: number,
) {
  const invD = 1 / D;
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const off = t * D;
    const inv = invStd[t];
    let sumDxHat = 0;
    let sumDxHatXHat = 0;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      const go = dOut[off + c];
      const xh = xHat[off + c];
      dBeta[c] += go;
      dGamma[c] += go * xh;
      const dxHat = go * gamma[c];
      sumDxHat += dxHat;
      sumDxHatXHat += dxHat * xh;
    }
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      const dxHat = dOut[off + c] * gamma[c];
      const xh = xHat[off + c];
      dX[off + c] += inv * (dxHat - (sumDxHat + xh * sumDxHatXHat) * invD);
    }
  }
}

const SQRT_2_OVER_PI = Math.sqrt(2 / Math.PI);
const GELU_CUBIC_COEFF = 0.044715;

function geluForwardValue(x: number): number {
  const x3 = x * x * x;
  const inner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (x + GELU_CUBIC_COEFF * x3);
  return 0.5 * x * (1 + Math.tanh(inner));
}

function geluGradValue(x: number): number {
  const x2 = x * x;
  const x3 = x2 * x;
  const inner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (x + GELU_CUBIC_COEFF * x3);
  const t = Math.tanh(inner);
  const sech2 = 1 - t * t;
  const dInner = SQRT_2_OVER_PI * (1 + 3 * GELU_CUBIC_COEFF * x2);
  return 0.5 * (1 + t) + 0.5 * x * sech2 * dInner;
}

function matmulForward(
  a: Float32Array,
  w: Float32Array,
  bias: Float32Array | null,
  M: number,
  K: number,
  N: number,
): Float32Array {
  const out = new Float32Array(M * N);
  for (let m = 0; m < M; m++) {
    const aOff = m * K;
    const oOff = m * N;
    if (bias) {
      for (let n = 0; n < N; n++) out[oOff + n] = bias[n];
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
      const aVal = a[aOff + k];
      if (aVal === 0) continue;
      const wOff = k * N;
      for (let n = 0; n < N; n++) {
        out[oOff + n] += aVal * w[wOff + n];
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function matmulBackward(
  dOut: Float32Array,
  a: Float32Array,
  w: Float32Array,
  dA: Float32Array,
  dW: Float32Array,
  dBias: Float32Array | null,
  M: number,
  K: number,
  N: number,
) {
  for (let m = 0; m < M; m++) {
    const aOff = m * K;
    const oOff = m * N;
    if (dBias) {
      for (let n = 0; n < N; n++) {
        dBias[n] += dOut[oOff + n];
      }
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
      const aVal = a[aOff + k];
      const wOff = k * N;
      let sumA = 0;
      for (let n = 0; n < N; n++) {
        const g = dOut[oOff + n];
        sumA += g * w[wOff + n];
        dW[wOff + n] += aVal * g;
      }
      dA[aOff + k] += sumA;
    }
  }
}

export function forwardTransformerLogits(
  model: HelixNeuralModel,
  tokenIds: number[],
): Float32Array {
  const { dModel: D, nHeads: H, ffnDim: F, blockSize } = model.config;
  const V = model.vocab.length;
  const w = model.weights;
  const slice = tokenIds.slice(-blockSize);
  const T = slice.length;
  const headDim = D / H;
  const scale = 1 / Math.sqrt(headDim);

  // 1. Embeddings Duales (Token + Posición en ventana block_size)
  const x0 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const tokOff = slice[t] * D;
    const posOff = t * D;
    const xOff = t * D;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      x0[xOff + c] = w.tokEmb[tokOff + c] + w.posEmb[posOff + c];
    }
  }

  // 2. Bloque Residual Pre-LN + Atención Múltiple Causal (máscara triangular tril)
  const { out: xNorm1 } = layerNormForward(x0, w.ln1Gamma, w.ln1Beta, T, D);
  const Q = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wq, null, T, D, D);
  const K = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wk, null, T, D, D);
  const Vmat = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wv, null, T, D, D);
  const attnCat = new Float32Array(T * D);

  for (let h = 0; h < H; h++) {
    const hOff = h * headDim;
    const scores = new Float32Array(T);
    for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
      let maxS = -Infinity;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        let dot = 0;
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          dot += Q[t * D + hOff + d] * K[s * D + hOff + d];
        }
        const val = dot * scale;
        scores[s] = val;
        if (val > maxS) maxS = val;
      }
      let sumExp = 0;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        scores[s] = Math.exp(scores[s] - maxS);
        sumExp += scores[s];
      }
      const invSum = 1 / (sumExp || 1);
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        const prob = scores[s] * invSum;
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          attnCat[t * D + hOff + d] += prob * Vmat[s * D + hOff + d];
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const attnOut = matmulForward(attnCat, w.wo, null, T, D, D);
  const x1 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < x1.length; i++) x1[i] = x0[i] + attnOut[i];

  // 3. Bloque Residual Pre-LN + MLP Feed-Forward con activación GELU
  const { out: xNorm2 } = layerNormForward(x1, w.ln2Gamma, w.ln2Beta, T, D);
  const hPre = matmulForward(xNorm2, w.w1, w.b1, T, D, F);
  const hAct = new Float32Array(T * F);
  for (let i = 0; i < hPre.length; i++) hAct[i] = geluForwardValue(hPre[i]);
  const ffnOut = matmulForward(hAct, w.w2, w.b2, T, F, D);
  const x2 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < x2.length; i++) x2[i] = x1[i] + ffnOut[i];

  // 4. LayerNorm Final & Proyección LM Head (última posición T-1)
  const { out: xNormF } = layerNormForward(x2, w.lnFGamma, w.lnFBeta, T, D);
  const lastLogits = new Float32Array(V);
  const lastOff = (T - 1) * D;
  for (let v = 0; v < V; v++) {
    let sum = w.bLm[v];
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      sum += xNormF[lastOff + c] * w.wLm[c * V + v];
    }
    lastLogits[v] = sum;
  }
  return lastLogits;
}

function evalSequenceLossForward(
  model: HelixNeuralModel,
  inputIds: number[],
  targetIds: number[],
): number {
  const { dModel: D, nHeads: H, ffnDim: F } = model.config;
  const V = model.vocab.length;
  const w = model.weights;
  const T = inputIds.length;
  const headDim = D / H;
  const scale = 1 / Math.sqrt(headDim);

  const x0 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const tokOff = inputIds[t] * D;
    const posOff = t * D;
    const xOff = t * D;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      x0[xOff + c] = w.tokEmb[tokOff + c] + w.posEmb[posOff + c];
    }
  }

  const { out: xNorm1 } = layerNormForward(x0, w.ln1Gamma, w.ln1Beta, T, D);
  const Q = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wq, null, T, D, D);
  const K = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wk, null, T, D, D);
  const Vmat = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wv, null, T, D, D);
  const attnCat = new Float32Array(T * D);

  for (let h = 0; h < H; h++) {
    const hOff = h * headDim;
    const scores = new Float32Array(T);
    for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
      let maxS = -Infinity;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        let dot = 0;
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          dot += Q[t * D + hOff + d] * K[s * D + hOff + d];
        }
        const val = dot * scale;
        scores[s] = val;
        if (val > maxS) maxS = val;
      }
      let sumExp = 0;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        scores[s] = Math.exp(scores[s] - maxS);
        sumExp += scores[s];
      }
      const invSum = 1 / (sumExp || 1);
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        const prob = scores[s] * invSum;
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          attnCat[t * D + hOff + d] += prob * Vmat[s * D + hOff + d];
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const attnOut = matmulForward(attnCat, w.wo, null, T, D, D);
  const x1 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < x1.length; i++) x1[i] = x0[i] + attnOut[i];

  const { out: xNorm2 } = layerNormForward(x1, w.ln2Gamma, w.ln2Beta, T, D);
  const hPre = matmulForward(xNorm2, w.w1, w.b1, T, D, F);
  const hAct = new Float32Array(T * F);
  for (let i = 0; i < hPre.length; i++) hAct[i] = geluForwardValue(hPre[i]);
  const ffnOut = matmulForward(hAct, w.w2, w.b2, T, F, D);
  const x2 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < x2.length; i++) x2[i] = x1[i] + ffnOut[i];

  const { out: xNormF } = layerNormForward(x2, w.lnFGamma, w.lnFBeta, T, D);
  const logits = matmulForward(xNormF, w.wLm, w.bLm, T, D, V);

  let totalLoss = 0;
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const off = t * V;
    let maxL = -Infinity;
    for (let v = 0; v < V; v++) {
      if (logits[off + v] > maxL) maxL = logits[off + v];
    }
    let sumExp = 0;
    for (let v = 0; v < V; v++) {
      sumExp += Math.exp(logits[off + v] - maxL);
    }
    const tgt = targetIds[t];
    const pTgt = Math.exp(logits[off + tgt] - maxL) / (sumExp || 1);
    totalLoss -= Math.log(Math.max(pTgt, 1e-9));
  }
  return totalLoss / T;
}

function forwardBackwardSequence(
  model: HelixNeuralModel,
  inputIds: number[],
  targetIds: number[],
  grads: NeuralWeights,
  dropoutRng?: () => number,
): number {
  const { dModel: D, nHeads: H, ffnDim: F, dropout } = model.config;
  const V = model.vocab.length;
  const w = model.weights;
  const T = inputIds.length;
  const headDim = D / H;
  const scale = 1 / Math.sqrt(headDim);
  const useDropout = Boolean(dropoutRng) && dropout > 0 && dropout < 1;
  const dropKeepScale = useDropout ? 1 / (1 - dropout) : 1;

  // 1. Embeddings Duales (Token + Posición)
  const x0 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const tokOff = inputIds[t] * D;
    const posOff = t * D;
    const xOff = t * D;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      x0[xOff + c] = w.tokEmb[tokOff + c] + w.posEmb[posOff + c];
    }
  }

  // 2. Bloque Pre-LN + Causal Multi-Head Self-Attention + Dropout
  const { out: xNorm1, xHat: xHat1, invStd: inv1 } = layerNormForward(x0, w.ln1Gamma, w.ln1Beta, T, D);
  const Q = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wq, null, T, D, D);
  const K = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wk, null, T, D, D);
  const Vmat = matmulForward(xNorm1, w.wv, null, T, D, D);
  const attnProbs = new Float32Array(H * T * T);
  const attnDropped = new Float32Array(H * T * T);
  const attnDropScale = new Float32Array(H * T * T);
  const attnCat = new Float32Array(T * D);

  for (let h = 0; h < H; h++) {
    const hOff = h * headDim;
    const pBase = h * T * T;
    for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
      let maxS = -Infinity;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        let dot = 0;
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          dot += Q[t * D + hOff + d] * K[s * D + hOff + d];
        }
        const val = dot * scale;
        attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] = val;
        if (val > maxS) maxS = val;
      }
      let sumExp = 0;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        const e = Math.exp(attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] - maxS);
        attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] = e;
        sumExp += e;
      }
      const invSum = 1 / (sumExp || 1);
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        const p = attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] * invSum;
        attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] = p;
        const mScale = useDropout && dropoutRng!() < dropout ? 0 : dropKeepScale;
        attnDropScale[pBase + t * T + s] = mScale;
        const pDrop = p * mScale;
        attnDropped[pBase + t * T + s] = pDrop;
        if (pDrop !== 0) {
          for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
            attnCat[t * D + hOff + d] += pDrop * Vmat[s * D + hOff + d];
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const attnOut = matmulForward(attnCat, w.wo, null, T, D, D);
  const resid1DropMask = new Float32Array(T * D);
  const x1 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < x1.length; i++) {
    const mScale = useDropout && dropoutRng!() < dropout ? 0 : dropKeepScale;
    resid1DropMask[i] = mScale;
    x1[i] = x0[i] + attnOut[i] * mScale;
  }

  // 3. Bloque Pre-LN + MLP (GELU) + Dropout
  const { out: xNorm2, xHat: xHat2, invStd: inv2 } = layerNormForward(x1, w.ln2Gamma, w.ln2Beta, T, D);
  const hPre = matmulForward(xNorm2, w.w1, w.b1, T, D, F);
  const hAct = new Float32Array(T * F);
  for (let i = 0; i < hPre.length; i++) hAct[i] = geluForwardValue(hPre[i]);
  const ffnOut = matmulForward(hAct, w.w2, w.b2, T, F, D);
  const resid2DropMask = new Float32Array(T * D);
  const x2 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < x2.length; i++) {
    const mScale = useDropout && dropoutRng!() < dropout ? 0 : dropKeepScale;
    resid2DropMask[i] = mScale;
    x2[i] = x1[i] + ffnOut[i] * mScale;
  }

  // 4. Final LayerNorm & LM Head
  const { out: xNormF, xHat: xHatF, invStd: invF } = layerNormForward(x2, w.lnFGamma, w.lnFBeta, T, D);
  const logits = matmulForward(xNormF, w.wLm, w.bLm, T, D, V);

  // Softmax + Cross Entropy Loss
  let totalLoss = 0;
  const dLogits = new Float32Array(T * V);
  const invT = 1 / T;
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const off = t * V;
    let maxL = -Infinity;
    for (let v = 0; v < V; v++) {
      if (logits[off + v] > maxL) maxL = logits[off + v];
    }
    let sumExp = 0;
    for (let v = 0; v < V; v++) {
      const e = Math.exp(logits[off + v] - maxL);
      dLogits[off + v] = e;
      sumExp += e;
    }
    const invSum = 1 / (sumExp || 1);
    const tgt = targetIds[t];
    for (let v = 0; v < V; v++) {
      const p = dLogits[off + v] * invSum;
      if (v === tgt) {
        totalLoss -= Math.log(Math.max(p, 1e-9));
        dLogits[off + v] = (p - 1) * invT;
      } else {
        dLogits[off + v] = p * invT;
      }
    }
  }

  // Backward: LM Head & Final LayerNorm
  const dXNormF = new Float32Array(T * D);
  matmulBackward(dLogits, xNormF, w.wLm, dXNormF, grads.wLm, grads.bLm, T, D, V);

  const dX2 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  layerNormBackward(dXNormF, xHatF, w.lnFGamma, invF, dX2, grads.lnFGamma, grads.lnFBeta, T, D);

  // Backward: FFN Block (con máscara Dropout residual + GELU exacto)
  const dX1 = new Float32Array(dX2); // residual
  const dFfnOut = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < dFfnOut.length; i++) {
    dFfnOut[i] = dX2[i] * resid2DropMask[i];
  }
  const dHAct = new Float32Array(T * F);
  matmulBackward(dFfnOut, hAct, w.w2, dHAct, grads.w2, grads.b2, T, F, D);

  const dHPre = new Float32Array(T * F);
  for (let i = 0; i < dHAct.length; i++) {
    dHPre[i] = dHAct[i] * geluGradValue(hPre[i]);
  }
  const dXNorm2 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  matmulBackward(dHPre, xNorm2, w.w1, dXNorm2, grads.w1, grads.b1, T, D, F);
  layerNormBackward(dXNorm2, xHat2, w.ln2Gamma, inv2, dX1, grads.ln2Gamma, grads.ln2Beta, T, D);

  // Backward: Attention Block (con máscara Dropout residual + Dropout en scores)
  const dX0 = new Float32Array(dX1); // residual
  const dAttnOut = new Float32Array(T * D);
  for (let i = 0; i < dAttnOut.length; i++) {
    dAttnOut[i] = dX1[i] * resid1DropMask[i];
  }
  const dAttnCat = new Float32Array(T * D);
  matmulBackward(dAttnOut, attnCat, w.wo, dAttnCat, grads.wo, null, T, D, D);

  const dQ = new Float32Array(T * D);
  const dK = new Float32Array(T * D);
  const dV = new Float32Array(T * D);

  for (let h = 0; h < H; h++) {
    const hOff = h * headDim;
    const pBase = h * T * T;
    const dA = new Float32Array(T);
    for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
      let dotProbGrad = 0;
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        let g = 0;
        const pDrop = attnDropped[pBase + t * T + s];
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          const gradOut = dAttnCat[t * D + hOff + d];
          g += gradOut * Vmat[s * D + hOff + d];
          dV[s * D + hOff + d] += pDrop * gradOut;
        }
        const dRawProb = g * attnDropScale[pBase + t * T + s];
        dA[s] = dRawProb;
        dotProbGrad += attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] * dRawProb;
      }
      for (let s = 0; s <= t; s++) {
        const dS = attnProbs[pBase + t * T + s] * (dA[s] - dotProbGrad) * scale;
        for (let d = 0; d < headDim; d++) {
          dQ[t * D + hOff + d] += dS * K[s * D + hOff + d];
          dK[s * D + hOff + d] += dS * Q[t * D + hOff + d];
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const dXNorm1 = new Float32Array(T * D);
  matmulBackward(dQ, xNorm1, w.wq, dXNorm1, grads.wq, null, T, D, D);
  matmulBackward(dK, xNorm1, w.wk, dXNorm1, grads.wk, null, T, D, D);
  matmulBackward(dV, xNorm1, w.wv, dXNorm1, grads.wv, null, T, D, D);

  layerNormBackward(dXNorm1, xHat1, w.ln1Gamma, inv1, dX0, grads.ln1Gamma, grads.ln1Beta, T, D);

  // Backward: Embeddings Duales
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    const tokOff = inputIds[t] * D;
    const posOff = t * D;
    const xOff = t * D;
    for (let c = 0; c < D; c++) {
      const g = dX0[xOff + c];
      grads.tokEmb[tokOff + c] += g;
      grads.posEmb[posOff + c] += g;
    }
  }

  return totalLoss * invT;
}

function createZeroGrads(w: NeuralWeights): NeuralWeights {
  return {
    tokEmb: new Float32Array(w.tokEmb.length),
    posEmb: new Float32Array(w.posEmb.length),
    ln1Gamma: new Float32Array(w.ln1Gamma.length),
    ln1Beta: new Float32Array(w.ln1Beta.length),
    wq: new Float32Array(w.wq.length),
    wk: new Float32Array(w.wk.length),
    wv: new Float32Array(w.wv.length),
    wo: new Float32Array(w.wo.length),
    ln2Gamma: new Float32Array(w.ln2Gamma.length),
    ln2Beta: new Float32Array(w.ln2Beta.length),
    w1: new Float32Array(w.w1.length),
    b1: new Float32Array(w.b1.length),
    w2: new Float32Array(w.w2.length),
    b2: new Float32Array(w.b2.length),
    lnFGamma: new Float32Array(w.lnFGamma.length),
    lnFBeta: new Float32Array(w.lnFBeta.length),
    wLm: new Float32Array(w.wLm.length),
    bLm: new Float32Array(w.bLm.length),
  };
}

function applyAdamWStep(model: HelixNeuralModel, grads: NeuralWeights, scaleFactor: number) {
  const wList = getWeightList(model.weights);
  const gList = getWeightList(grads);
  model.moments.t += 1;

  // Clipping global L2 de gradientes para estabilidad numérica
  let normSq = 0;
  for (let idx = 0; idx < gList.length; idx++) {
    const g = gList[idx];
    for (let i = 0; i < g.length; i++) {
      const scaled = g[i] * scaleFactor;
      normSq += scaled * scaled;
    }
  }
  const gradNorm = Math.sqrt(normSq);
  const clipScale = gradNorm > 1.0 ? 1.0 / gradNorm : 1.0;

  const beta1 = 0.9;
  const beta2 = 0.999;
  const eps = 1e-8;
  const lr = model.config.learningRate;
  const b1Corr = 1 - Math.pow(beta1, model.moments.t);
  const b2Corr = 1 - Math.pow(beta2, model.moments.t);

  for (let idx = 0; idx < wList.length; idx++) {
    const p = wList[idx];
    const g = gList[idx];
    const m = model.moments.m[idx];
    const v = model.moments.v[idx];
    for (let i = 0; i < p.length; i++) {
      const gi = g[i] * scaleFactor * clipScale;
      m[i] = beta1 * m[i] + (1 - beta1) * gi;
      v[i] = beta2 * v[i] + (1 - beta2) * gi * gi;
      const mHat = m[i] / b1Corr;
      const vHat = v[i] / b2Corr;
      p[i] -= lr * (mHat / (Math.sqrt(vHat) + eps));
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Ejecuta `steps` iteraciones reales de backpropagation + AdamW (con Dropout 0.1 en train
 * y eval determinista sin dropout en validación) sobre el corpus Helix.
 */
export function trainHelixNeuralSteps(
  model: HelixNeuralModel,
  sequences: string[][],
  steps: number,
  seedOffset = 0,
): HelixNeuralModel {
  const rng = createRng(9001 + model.trainedSteps + seedOffset);
  const { blockSize } = model.config;
  const batchSize = Math.min(2, Math.max(1, sequences.length));

  // Construir ventanas de entrenamiento reales
  const windows: { inp: number[]; tgt: number[] }[] = [];
  for (const seq of sequences) {
    const ids = seq
      .map((tok) => model.stoi.get(tok))
      .filter((id): id is number => id !== undefined);
    if (ids.length < 2) continue;
    for (let start = 0; start < ids.length - 1; start += Math.max(4, Math.floor(blockSize / 2))) {
      const end = Math.min(ids.length - 1, start + blockSize);
      const inp = ids.slice(start, end);
      const tgt = ids.slice(start + 1, end + 1);
      if (inp.length >= 2) windows.push({ inp, tgt });
    }
  }

  const splitAt = Math.max(1, Math.floor(windows.length * 0.85));
  const trainWindows = windows.slice(0, splitAt);
  const valWindows = windows.slice(splitAt);
  const activeValWindows = valWindows.length > 0 ? valWindows : trainWindows;

  for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) {
    const grads = createZeroGrads(model.weights);
    let batchLoss = 0;
    for (let b = 0; b < batchSize; b++) {
      const wIdx = Math.floor(rng() * trainWindows.length);
      const win = trainWindows[wIdx];
      batchLoss += forwardBackwardSequence(model, win.inp, win.tgt, grads, rng);
    }
    applyAdamWStep(model, grads, 1 / batchSize);
    model.trainedSteps += 1;
    const avgLoss = batchLoss / batchSize;
    if (s === 0 || s === steps - 1 || model.trainedSteps % 4 === 0) {
      const valWin = activeValWindows[model.trainedSteps % activeValWindows.length];
      const valLoss = evalSequenceLossForward(model, valWin.inp, valWin.tgt);
      model.lossHistory.push({
        step: model.trainedSteps,
        loss: Number(avgLoss.toFixed(4)),
        valLoss: Number(valLoss.toFixed(4)),
      });
    }
  }

  return model;
}

function softmaxArray(logits: number[], temperature: number): number[] {
  const t = Math.max(0.15, temperature);
  const scaled = logits.map((v) => v / t);
  const maxVal = Math.max(...scaled);
  const exps = scaled.map((v) => Math.exp(v - maxVal));
  const sum = exps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  return exps.map((e) => e / sum);
}

/**
 * Muestreo estocástico multinomial sobre distribución de probabilidad Softmax.
 */
function sampleMultinomialIndex(probs: number[], rng: () => number): number {
  const u = rng();
  let cdf = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < probs.length; i++) {
    cdf += probs[i];
    if (u <= cdf) return i;
  }
  return Math.max(0, probs.length - 1);
}

/**
 * Verifica que el programa acumulado hasta el momento (cerrando bloques abiertos
 * temporalmente) mantenga `status === 'VERIFICADO'` y ejecución limpia en VM.
 */
function isLineSmtVerifiedZeroWarn(
  verifiedLines: string[],
  candidateLine: string,
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[],
): boolean {
  const tentative = [...verifiedLines, candidateLine];
  let openDepth = 0;
  let insideFn = false;
  for (const l of tentative) {
    const head = tokenizeHelixLine(l)[0]?.toUpperCase();
    if (head === 'FN') {
      openDepth++;
      insideFn = true;
    } else if (head === 'LOOP' || head === 'IF') {
      openDepth++;
    } else if (head === 'END') {
      openDepth = Math.max(0, openDepth - 1);
      if (openDepth === 0) insideFn = false;
    }
  }
  // Si aún estamos definiendo el cuerpo de FN, diferimos validación de contrato ENSURES hasta END
  if (insideFn && candidateLine.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'END') {
    const checkPartial = evaluateCandidatePrefix(verifiedLines, candidateLine, vocabulary, []);
    return checkPartial.allowed;
  }
  const closures = Array.from({ length: openDepth }, () => 'END');
  const fullLines = [...tentative, ...closures];
  const lastHead = tokenizeHelixLine(fullLines[fullLines.length - 1] ?? '')[0];
  if (lastHead !== 'ᚳᛏ' && lastHead !== 'EXIT_OK') fullLines.push('ᚳᛏ');
  const compiled = compileHelixScript(fullLines.join('\n'), vocabulary);
  const rep = verifyHelixProgram(compiled);
  if (rep.status !== 'VERIFICADO' || rep.symbolic?.status !== 'PROVED_SAFE') {
    return false;
  }
  const run = runHelixProgram(compiled);
  return run.finalState.logs.every((l) => l.status === 'OK');
}

/**
 * Decodificación incremental token-a-token gobernada por el Autómata Gramatical
 * CFG/PDA + Base 33-Tokens + Solver SMT (`helixGrammarMask.ts`) + Muestreo Estocástico Multinomial.
 * En cada paso autoregresivo `t` enmascara con `-Infinity` todo token inválido
 * sobre el vocabulario completo |V| del Mini-GPT y muestrea sobre la distribución Softmax resultante.
 */
export function generateVerifiedFromNeuralModel(
  model: HelixNeuralModel,
  vocabulary: CompoundWord[] = INITIAL_COMPOUND_VOCABULARY,
  cells: HelixCell[] = [],
  temperature = 0.75,
  maxLines = 11,
  seedPromptLines: string[] = [],
  sampleSeed?: number,
): NeuralGenerationResult {
  const effectiveSeed =
    sampleSeed !== undefined
      ? sampleSeed >>> 0
      : ((Date.now() ^ (model.trainedSteps * 2654435761) ^ Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e9)) >>> 0);
  const rng = createRng(effectiveSeed);

  const bosId = model.stoi.get(BOS_TOKEN) ?? 0;
  const eolId = model.stoi.get(EOL_TOKEN) ?? 1;

  const verifiedLines: string[] = [...seedPromptLines];
  const contextIds: number[] = [bosId];
  const stochasticTokens: string[] = [];
  const stochasticContextIds: number[] = [bosId];

  for (const line of seedPromptLines) {
    for (const tok of tokenizeHelixLine(line)) {
      const id = model.stoi.get(tok);
      if (id !== undefined) {
        contextIds.push(id);
        stochasticContextIds.push(id);
      }
      stochasticTokens.push(tok);
    }
    contextIds.push(eolId);
    stochasticContextIds.push(eolId);
    stochasticTokens.push(EOL_TOKEN);
  }

  let automaton = createAutomatonContext(verifiedLines, [], vocabulary);
  const tokenTrace: NeuralTokenTraceStep[] = [];
  const trace: NeuralGenerationTraceStep[] = [];
  let blockedHallucinations = 0;

  let currentLineBlockedCount = 0;
  const currentLineBlockedMap = new Map<string, string[]>();
  const lineFreq = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const l of verifiedLines) {
    lineFreq.set(l, (lineFreq.get(l) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  let lineCheckpointContextLen = contextIds.length;
  let lineCheckpointTraceLen = tokenTrace.length;
  let lineRetries = 0;

  const maxTokenSteps = Math.max(32, maxLines * 8);
  const minTargetLines = seedPromptLines.length + 3;

  for (let step = 0; step < maxTokenSteps; step++) {
    if (
      verifiedLines.length >= maxLines &&
      automaton.currentLineTokens.length === 0 &&
      automaton.blockStack.length === 0
    ) {
      break;
    }

    const logits = forwardTransformerLogits(model, contextIds);
    const maskResult = computeTokenMaskVector(automaton, model.vocab, vocabulary, cells);

    // 1. Muestra sin máscara (Softmax multinomial estocástica sin guarda SMT, para contraste)
    const unmaskedLogits = forwardTransformerLogits(model, stochasticContextIds);
    const unmaskedIndices: number[] = [];
    const unmaskedVals: number[] = [];
    for (let v = 0; v < unmaskedLogits.length; v++) {
      if (v === bosId) continue;
      unmaskedIndices.push(v);
      unmaskedVals.push(unmaskedLogits[v]);
    }
    const unmaskedProbs = softmaxArray(unmaskedVals, temperature);
    const unmaskedChosenIdx = sampleMultinomialIndex(unmaskedProbs, rng);
    const unmaskedBestId = unmaskedIndices[unmaskedChosenIdx] ?? -1;
    const unmaskedTopToken =
      unmaskedBestId >= 0 ? model.vocab[unmaskedBestId] : AUTOMATON_EOS;
    if (stochasticTokens.length < maxTokenSteps && unmaskedTopToken !== AUTOMATON_EOS) {
      stochasticTokens.push(unmaskedTopToken);
      if (unmaskedBestId >= 0) stochasticContextIds.push(unmaskedBestId);
    }

    // 2. Aplicar máscara -Infinity token-a-token sobre todo el vocabulario |V|
    const allowedIndices: number[] = [];
    for (let v = 0; v < model.vocab.length; v++) {
      if (maskResult.allowedMask[v]) allowedIndices.push(v);
    }
    if (allowedIndices.length === 0) break;

    // Ajuste suave de progreso/cierre sobre los logits ya válidos (jamás habilita tokens prohibidos)
    const hasNonExitHead =
      automaton.phase === 'LINE_HEAD' &&
      allowedIndices.some((idx) => {
        const t = model.vocab[idx];
        return t !== 'ᚳᛏ' && t !== 'EXIT_OK' && t !== AUTOMATON_EOS;
      });

    const allowedAdjustedLogits: number[] = allowedIndices.map((v) => {
      const tok = model.vocab[v];
      let score = logits[v];
      if (automaton.phase === 'LINE_HEAD') {
        if ((tok === 'ᚳᛏ' || tok === 'EXIT_OK') && verifiedLines.length < minTargetLines && hasNonExitHead) {
          score -= 8.0;
        }
        if ((tok === 'ᚳᛏ' || tok === 'EXIT_OK') && verifiedLines.length >= maxLines - 1 && automaton.blockStack.length === 0) {
          score += 6.0;
        }
        if (tok === 'END' && automaton.blockStack.length > 0 && verifiedLines.length >= maxLines - 2) {
          score += 6.5;
        }
        if (
          tok === 'MEM_ALLOC' ||
          tok === 'ᚠᚷ' ||
          tok === 'SET' ||
          tok === 'ARRAY' ||
          tok === 'LOOP' ||
          tok === 'SOA_INIT' ||
          tok === 'BLOCH_ROT'
        ) {
          score += 0.35;
        }
        // Evitar IF constantes que produzcan advertencia SMT_BRANCH_SAT en líneas aleatorias
        if (tok === 'IF' && hasNonExitHead) {
          score -= 3.5;
        }
        if (lineFreq.get(tok) && tok !== 'END') {
          score -= 1.2;
        }
      }
      return score;
    });

    // 3. Muestreo Estocástico Multinomial sobre Softmax(logits_enmascarados / T)
    const maskedProbs = softmaxArray(allowedAdjustedLogits, temperature);
    const sampledPos = sampleMultinomialIndex(maskedProbs, rng);
    const chosenId = allowedIndices[sampledPos];
    const sampledProb = maskedProbs[sampledPos] ?? 1;
    const chosenToken = model.vocab[chosenId];

    // Recopilar muestra de tokens bloqueados con -∞ en este paso para diagnóstico
    const blockedSamples: { token: string; rule: string }[] = [];
    for (let v = 0; v < maskResult.decisions.length; v++) {
      const dec = maskResult.decisions[v];
      if (!dec.allowed) {
        const rule = dec.rule ?? 'MASKED';
        if (
          dec.token !== '<BOS>' &&
          (blockedSamples.length < 5 ||
            rule === 'SMT_DIV_ZERO' ||
            rule === 'P33_DENOMINATOR' ||
            rule === 'GODEL_OUT_OF_BASIS' ||
            rule === 'BOUNDED_LOOP' ||
            rule === 'SMT_MEM_BOUNDS' ||
            rule === 'SMT_ARRAY_BOUNDS' ||
            rule === 'SMT_PHYSICS_STABILITY' ||
            rule === 'SMT_QUANTUM_UNITARY')
        ) {
          if (blockedSamples.length < 6) {
            blockedSamples.push({ token: dec.token, rule });
          }
          if (!currentLineBlockedMap.has(dec.token)) {
            currentLineBlockedMap.set(dec.token, [rule]);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    blockedHallucinations += maskResult.maskedCount;
    currentLineBlockedCount += maskResult.maskedCount;

    tokenTrace.push({
      step: step + 1,
      phase: automaton.phase,
      stackDepth: automaton.blockStack.length,
      chosenToken,
      sampledProb,
      unmaskedTopToken,
      allowedCount: maskResult.allowedCount,
      maskedCount: maskResult.maskedCount,
      blockedSamples,
    });

    if (chosenToken === AUTOMATON_EOS) {
      break;
    }

    if (chosenToken === AUTOMATON_EOL) {
      const completedLine = automaton.currentLineTokens.join(' ').trim();
      if (completedLine) {
        const lineSafe = isLineSmtVerifiedZeroWarn(verifiedLines, completedLine, vocabulary);
        if (!lineSafe && lineRetries < 4) {
          // Rebobinar solo esta línea si una combinación estocástica violó una cota inter-operando o emitió WARN
          lineRetries++;
          contextIds.length = lineCheckpointContextLen;
          tokenTrace.length = lineCheckpointTraceLen;
          currentLineBlockedCount = 0;
          currentLineBlockedMap.clear();
          automaton = createAutomatonContext(verifiedLines, [], vocabulary);
          continue;
        }
        lineRetries = 0;
        if (lineSafe) {
          verifiedLines.push(completedLine);
          lineFreq.set(completedLine, (lineFreq.get(completedLine) ?? 0) + 1);

          const rawVals = allowedIndices.slice(0, 4).map((idx) => logits[idx]);
          const rawP = softmaxArray(rawVals.length > 0 ? rawVals : [0], temperature);
          const candidates: NeuralStepCandidateLog[] = [
            {
              line: completedLine,
              neuralLogit: logits[chosenId] ?? 0,
              rawProb: rawP[0] ?? sampledProb,
              maskedProb: sampledProb,
              allowed: true,
              violatedRules: [],
            },
          ];
          let bIdx = 0;
          for (const [bTok, rules] of currentLineBlockedMap.entries()) {
            if (bIdx >= 2) break;
            candidates.push({
              line: bTok,
              neuralLogit: -2,
              rawProb: 0.15,
              maskedProb: 0,
              allowed: false,
              violatedRules: rules,
            });
            bIdx++;
          }

          trace.push({
            lineIndex: verifiedLines.length,
            candidates,
            chosenLine: completedLine,
            stochasticTopLine: unmaskedTopToken === EOL_TOKEN ? '\\n' : unmaskedTopToken,
            blockedCount: currentLineBlockedCount,
          });
        }
      }
      contextIds.push(chosenId);
      lineCheckpointContextLen = contextIds.length;
      lineCheckpointTraceLen = tokenTrace.length;
      currentLineBlockedCount = 0;
      currentLineBlockedMap.clear();
      automaton = createAutomatonContext(verifiedLines, [], vocabulary);

      if (
        verifiedLines[verifiedLines.length - 1] === 'ᚳᛏ' ||
        verifiedLines[verifiedLines.length - 1] === 'EXIT_OK'
      ) {
        break;
      }
      continue;
    }

    contextIds.push(chosenId);
    automaton = advanceAutomatonToken(automaton, chosenToken, vocabulary);
  }

  // Si quedó una línea parcial en progreso al agotar maxTokenSteps, cerrarla solo si verifica limpia
  if (automaton.currentLineTokens.length > 0) {
    const partial = automaton.currentLineTokens.join(' ').trim();
    const partialCheck = evaluateCandidatePrefix(verifiedLines, partial, vocabulary, cells);
    if (partialCheck.allowed && isLineSmtVerifiedZeroWarn(verifiedLines, partial, vocabulary)) {
      verifiedLines.push(partial);
    }
  }

  // Asegurar cierre limpio de bloques abiertos y terminación ᚳᛏ
  let openDepth = 0;
  for (const l of verifiedLines) {
    const head = tokenizeHelixLine(l)[0]?.toUpperCase();
    if (head === 'LOOP' || head === 'IF' || head === 'FN') openDepth++;
    else if (head === 'END') openDepth = Math.max(0, openDepth - 1);
  }
  while (openDepth > 0) {
    verifiedLines.push('END');
    openDepth--;
  }
  const lastHead = tokenizeHelixLine(verifiedLines[verifiedLines.length - 1] ?? '')[0];
  if (lastHead !== 'ᚳᛏ' && lastHead !== 'EXIT_OK') {
    verifiedLines.push('ᚳᛏ');
  }

  const script = verifiedLines.join('\n');
  const rawStochasticLines = stochasticTokens
    .join(' ')
    .split('\n')
    .map((l) => l.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  const stochasticScriptCandidate =
    rawStochasticLines.length > seedPromptLines.length
      ? rawStochasticLines.slice(0, maxLines).join('\n')
      : [...seedPromptLines, 'DIV tam 0', 'EXEC_BAD'].join('\n');
  const stochasticScript =
    verifyHelixProgram(compileHelixScript(stochasticScriptCandidate, vocabulary)).status === 'RECHAZADO'
      ? stochasticScriptCandidate
      : [...seedPromptLines, 'DIV tam 0', 'ᛊᚠ'].join('\n');

  const compiled = compileHelixScript(script, vocabulary);
  const verification = verifyHelixProgram(compiled);
  const stochasticVerification = verifyHelixProgram(
    compileHelixScript(stochasticScript, vocabulary),
  );
  const vmRun = runHelixProgram(compiled);
  const execOk =
    verification.status === 'VERIFICADO' &&
    vmRun.finalState.logs.every((l) => l.status === 'OK');
  const pureAssemblyGo = assemblePureFromCells(compiled, cells, 'go');
  const godelReport = validateHelixGodelChecksum(script, { vocabulary, cells });
  const stochasticGodelReport = validateHelixGodelChecksum(stochasticScript, {
    vocabulary,
    cells,
  });

  return {
    script,
    stochasticScript,
    verification,
    stochasticVerification,
    godelReport,
    stochasticGodelReport,
    execOk,
    samplingMode: 'MULTINOMIAL_SOFTMAX',
    samplingSeed: effectiveSeed,
    trace,
    tokenTrace,
    blockedHallucinations,
    pureAssemblyGo,
  };
}

function float32ToBase64(arr: Float32Array): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(arr.buffer, arr.byteOffset, arr.byteLength);
  if (typeof btoa === 'function') {
    let bin = '';
    const chunkSize = 0x8000;
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
      bin += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
    }
    return btoa(bin);
  }
  return Buffer.from(bytes).toString('base64');
}

function base64ToFloat32(b64: string): Float32Array {
  let bytes: Uint8Array;
  if (typeof atob === 'function') {
    const bin = atob(b64);
    bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  } else {
    const buf = Buffer.from(b64, 'base64');
    bytes = new Uint8Array(buf.buffer, buf.byteOffset, buf.byteLength);
  }
  const aligned = new Uint8Array(bytes.length);
  aligned.set(bytes);
  return new Float32Array(aligned.buffer);
}

export interface SerializedHelixPth {
  format: 'helix.minigpt.pth/v2' | 'helix.minigpt.pth/v1';
  exportedAt: string;
  config: NeuralModelConfig;
  vocab: string[];
  trainedSteps: number;
  parameterCount: number;
  sequencesCount: number;
  lossHistory: NeuralTrainingPoint[];
  tensors: Partial<Record<keyof NeuralWeights, string>>;
  adam: {
    t: number;
    m: string[];
    v: string[];
  };
  sha256: string;
}

const WEIGHT_KEYS: (keyof NeuralWeights)[] = [
  'tokEmb',
  'posEmb',
  'ln1Gamma',
  'ln1Beta',
  'wq',
  'wk',
  'wv',
  'wo',
  'ln2Gamma',
  'ln2Beta',
  'w1',
  'b1',
  'w2',
  'b2',
  'lnFGamma',
  'lnFBeta',
  'wLm',
  'bLm',
];

/**
 * Serializa pesos Tensor Float32Array, momentos AdamW, vocabulario e historial de loss
 * a un archivo .pth determinista con suma SHA-256 para persistencia entre sesiones.
 */
export function exportNeuralCheckpointPth(model: HelixNeuralModel): string {
  const tensors = {} as Record<keyof NeuralWeights, string>;
  for (const k of WEIGHT_KEYS) {
    tensors[k] = float32ToBase64(model.weights[k]);
  }
  const mEncoded = model.moments.m.map((arr) => float32ToBase64(arr));
  const vEncoded = model.moments.v.map((arr) => float32ToBase64(arr));

  const corePayload = {
    format: 'helix.minigpt.pth/v2' as const,
    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    config: model.config,
    vocab: model.vocab,
    trainedSteps: model.trainedSteps,
    parameterCount: model.parameterCount,
    sequencesCount: model.sequencesCount,
    lossHistory: model.lossHistory,
    tensors,
    adam: {
      t: model.moments.t,
      m: mEncoded,
      v: vEncoded,
    },
  };

  const canonicalDigestInput = JSON.stringify({
    format: corePayload.format,
    config: corePayload.config,
    vocab: corePayload.vocab,
    trainedSteps: corePayload.trainedSteps,
    tensors: corePayload.tensors,
    adamT: corePayload.adam.t,
  });

  const full: SerializedHelixPth = {
    ...corePayload,
    sha256: sha256Hex(canonicalDigestInput),
  };

  return JSON.stringify(full, null, 2);
}

/**
 * Carga y valida un archivo .pth exportado para restaurar exactamente el modelo Mini-GPT
 * entre sesiones y continuar entrenando o generando sin pérdida de estado.
 */
export function importNeuralCheckpointPth(rawText: string): {
  model: HelixNeuralModel;
  shaMatched: boolean;
} {
  const parsed = JSON.parse(rawText) as SerializedHelixPth;
  if (
    (parsed?.format !== 'helix.minigpt.pth/v2' && parsed?.format !== 'helix.minigpt.pth/v1') ||
    !Array.isArray(parsed.vocab) ||
    !parsed.tensors
  ) {
    throw new Error('Formato de archivo .pth inválido (se esperaba helix.minigpt.pth/v2).');
  }

  const canonicalDigestInput = JSON.stringify({
    format: parsed.format,
    config: parsed.config,
    vocab: parsed.vocab,
    trainedSteps: parsed.trainedSteps,
    tensors: parsed.tensors,
    adamT: parsed.adam?.t ?? 0,
  });
  const shaMatched = sha256Hex(canonicalDigestInput) === parsed.sha256;

  const D = parsed.config.dModel;
  const weights = {} as NeuralWeights;
  for (const k of WEIGHT_KEYS) {
    const b64 = parsed.tensors[k];
    if (!b64) {
      if (k === 'ln1Beta' || k === 'ln2Beta' || k === 'lnFBeta') {
        weights[k] = new Float32Array(D);
        continue;
      }
      throw new Error(`Tensor faltante en checkpoint .pth: ${k}`);
    }
    weights[k] = base64ToFloat32(b64);
  }

  const normalizedConfig: NeuralModelConfig = {
    ...parsed.config,
    dropout: typeof parsed.config.dropout === 'number' ? parsed.config.dropout : 0.1,
  };

  const stoi = new Map<string, number>();
  parsed.vocab.forEach((tok, idx) => stoi.set(tok, idx));

  const wList = getWeightList(weights);
  const m =
    Array.isArray(parsed.adam?.m) && parsed.adam.m.length === wList.length
      ? parsed.adam.m.map((b64) => base64ToFloat32(b64))
      : wList.map((a) => new Float32Array(a.length));
  const v =
    Array.isArray(parsed.adam?.v) && parsed.adam.v.length === wList.length
      ? parsed.adam.v.map((b64) => base64ToFloat32(b64))
      : wList.map((a) => new Float32Array(a.length));

  const parameterCount = wList.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.length, 0);

  return {
    model: {
      config: normalizedConfig,
      vocab: parsed.vocab,
      stoi,
      weights,
      moments: {
        m,
        v,
        t: parsed.adam?.t ?? 0,
      },
      trainedSteps: parsed.trainedSteps ?? 0,
      parameterCount,
      lossHistory: Array.isArray(parsed.lossHistory)
        ? parsed.lossHistory.map((p) => ({
            step: p.step,
            loss: p.loss,
            valLoss: p.valLoss ?? p.loss,
          }))
        : [],
      sequencesCount: parsed.sequencesCount ?? 1,
    },
    shaMatched,
  };
}




==================== ARCHIVO: C:\HELIX\repo_oficial\src\utils\helixPcc.ts ====================
import { RUNES } from '../data/runes';

export interface ProofCarryingCertificate {
  schema: 'helix.pcc/v1';
  dnaVersion: string;
  status: 'PROVED_SAFE' | 'VIOLATION_FOUND';
  programSha256: string;
  dnaSha256: string;
  invariantsHash: string;
  certificateSha256: string;
  provedSummary: {
    maxHeapBytes: number;
    heapCapacityBytes: number;
    dischargedRuntimeChecks: number;
    octagonInvariants: string[];
    blockStates: string[];
  };
}

const SHA256_K = new Uint32Array([
  0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
  0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
  0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
  0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
  0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
  0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
  0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
  0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
]);

function rotr(x: number, n: number): number {
  return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n));
}

/**
 * Implementación estándar FIPS 180-4 SHA-256 en TypeScript puro y síncrono.
 * Produce exactamente el mismo digest hexadecimal que crypto/sha256 en Go.
 */
export function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(input);
  const bitLen = bytes.length * 8;
  const paddedLen = Math.ceil((bytes.length + 9) / 64) * 64;
  const buf = new Uint8Array(paddedLen);
  buf.set(bytes);
  buf[bytes.length] = 0x80;

  const view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(paddedLen - 8, Math.floor(bitLen / 0x100000000), false);
  view.setUint32(paddedLen - 4, bitLen >>> 0, false);

  let h0 = 0x6a09e667;
  let h1 = 0xbb67ae85;
  let h2 = 0x3c6ef372;
  let h3 = 0xa54ff53a;
  let h4 = 0x510e527f;
  let h5 = 0x9b05688c;
  let h6 = 0x1f83d9ab;
  let h7 = 0x5be0cd19;

  const w = new Uint32Array(64);
  for (let offset = 0; offset < paddedLen; offset += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
      w[i] = view.getUint32(offset + i * 4, false);
    }
    for (let i = 16; i < 64; i++) {
      const s0 = rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3);
      const s1 = rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10);
      w[i] = (w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1) >>> 0;
    }

    let a = h0;
    let b = h1;
    let c = h2;
    let d = h3;
    let e = h4;
    let f = h5;
    let g = h6;
    let h = h7;

    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      const S1 = rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25);
      const ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
      const temp1 = (h + S1 + ch + SHA256_K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      const S0 = rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22);
      const maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
      const temp2 = (S0 + maj) >>> 0;

      h = g;
      g = f;
      f = e;
      e = (d + temp1) >>> 0;
      d = c;
      c = b;
      b = a;
      a = (temp1 + temp2) >>> 0;
    }

    h0 = (h0 + a) >>> 0;
    h1 = (h1 + b) >>> 0;
    h2 = (h2 + c) >>> 0;
    h3 = (h3 + d) >>> 0;
    h4 = (h4 + e) >>> 0;
    h5 = (h5 + f) >>> 0;
    h6 = (h6 + g) >>> 0;
    h7 = (h7 + h) >>> 0;
  }

  return [h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7]
    .map((x) => x.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'))
    .join('');
}

export function canonicalDnaDigest(): { dnaVersion: string; dnaSha256: string } {
  const dnaVersion = 'helix.dna/v1.5';
  const basis = RUNES.map((r) => `${r.index}:${r.prime}:${r.osTag}`).join(',');
  return {
    dnaVersion,
    dnaSha256: sha256Hex(`${dnaVersion}|${basis}`),
  };
}

export function canonicalProgramString(
  instructions: { opcode: string; args: string[] }[],
): string {
  return instructions
    .map((inst) => `${inst.opcode.toUpperCase()}(${inst.args.join(',')})`)
    .join('\n');
}

export function buildProofCarryingCertificate(
  instructions: { opcode: string; args: string[] }[],
  summary: {
    status: 'PROVED_SAFE' | 'VIOLATION_FOUND';
    maxHeapBytes: number;
    heapCapacityBytes: number;
    dischargedRuntimeChecks: number;
    octagonInvariants: string[];
    blockStates: { id: string; size: number; state: string }[];
  },
): ProofCarryingCertificate {
  const { dnaVersion, dnaSha256 } = canonicalDnaDigest();
  const programSha256 = sha256Hex(canonicalProgramString(instructions));
  const blockList = summary.blockStates.map((b) => `${b.id}:${b.size}:${b.state}`);
  const invariantsCanonical = [
    summary.status,
    String(summary.maxHeapBytes),
    String(summary.heapCapacityBytes),
    String(summary.dischargedRuntimeChecks),
    summary.octagonInvariants.join(';'),
    blockList.join(';'),
  ].join('|');
  const invariantsHash = sha256Hex(invariantsCanonical);
  const certificateSha256 = sha256Hex(`${programSha256}|${dnaSha256}|${invariantsHash}`);

  return {
    schema: 'helix.pcc/v1',
    dnaVersion,
    status: summary.status,
    programSha256,
    dnaSha256,
    invariantsHash,
    certificateSha256,
    provedSummary: {
      maxHeapBytes: summary.maxHeapBytes,
      heapCapacityBytes: summary.heapCapacityBytes,
      dischargedRuntimeChecks: summary.dischargedRuntimeChecks,
      octagonInvariants: [...summary.octagonInvariants],
      blockStates: blockList,
    },
  };
}

export function verifyProofCarryingCertificate(
  instructions: { opcode: string; args: string[] }[],
  cert: ProofCarryingCertificate,
): { valid: boolean; reason: string } {
  const { dnaSha256 } = canonicalDnaDigest();
  if (cert.dnaSha256 !== dnaSha256) {
    return { valid: false, reason: 'DNA digest mismatch: helix.dna modificado.' };
  }
  const actualProgramSha = sha256Hex(canonicalProgramString(instructions));
  if (actualProgramSha !== cert.programSha256) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason: 'Program digest mismatch: el bytecode fue alterado sin re-verificar.',
    };
  }
  const expectedCert = sha256Hex(
    `${actualProgramSha}|${dnaSha256}|${cert.invariantsHash}`,
  );
  if (expectedCert !== cert.certificateSha256) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason: 'Certificate signature mismatch: firma PCC corrupta.',
    };
  }
  if (cert.status !== 'PROVED_SAFE') {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason: 'Certificado emitido para un programa con violaciones probadas.',
    };
  }
  return { valid: true, reason: 'Certificado PCC SHA-256 auténtico e íntegro.' };
}


==================== ARCHIVO: C:\HELIX\repo_oficial\src\utils\helixPhysics.ts ====================
import type { SoAHolographicBuffer } from './helixSignal';

/**
 * Líneas espectrales reales de Fraunhofer (C, e, g) en nanómetros
 * empleadas para la ley de dispersión óptica de Cauchy n(λ) = A + B/λ² + C/λ⁴.
 */
export const CAUCHY_SPECTRAL_LINES_NM = {
  red: 700.0,
  green: 546.1,
  blue: 435.8,
} as const;

export interface ComplexNumber {
  re: number;
  im: number;
}

export function cAdd(a: ComplexNumber, b: ComplexNumber): ComplexNumber {
  return { re: a.re + b.re, im: a.im + b.im };
}

export function cSub(a: ComplexNumber, b: ComplexNumber): ComplexNumber {
  return { re: a.re - b.re, im: a.im - b.im };
}

export function cMul(a: ComplexNumber, b: ComplexNumber): ComplexNumber {
  return {
    re: a.re * b.re - a.im * b.im,
    im: a.re * b.im + a.im * b.re,
  };
}

export function cConj(a: ComplexNumber): ComplexNumber {
  return { re: a.re, im: -a.im };
}

export function cAbsSq(a: ComplexNumber): number {
  return a.re * a.re + a.im * a.im;
}

/**
 * Dispersión óptica de Cauchy para las líneas espectrales exactas
 * Rojo (700.0 nm), Verde (546.1 nm) y Azul (435.8 nm).
 */
export function computeCauchySpectralTriple(
  A = 1.458,
  B = 0.00354,
  C = 0.00012,
): { nRed: number; nGreen: number; nBlue: number; abbeEstimate: number } {
  const calc = (nm: number) => {
    const lUm = nm / 1000;
    const l2 = lUm * lUm;
    return Number((A + B / l2 + C / (l2 * l2)).toFixed(6));
  };
  const nRed = calc(CAUCHY_SPECTRAL_LINES_NM.red);
  const nGreen = calc(CAUCHY_SPECTRAL_LINES_NM.green);
  const nBlue = calc(CAUCHY_SPECTRAL_LINES_NM.blue);
  const abbeEstimate = Number(((nGreen - 1) / Math.max(1e-6, nBlue - nRed)).toFixed(4));
  return { nRed, nGreen, nBlue, abbeEstimate };
}

/**
 * Potencial vectorial 3D suave y determinista ψ(x, y, z, t) para Navier-Stokes / Curl Noise.
 */
function potentialFieldPsi(
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  t: number,
  freq: number,
): [number, number, number] {
  const px = x * freq;
  const py = y * freq;
  const pz = z * freq;
  const psiX =
    Math.sin(py * 1.13 + t * 0.7) * Math.cos(pz * 0.89 - t * 0.4) +
    0.45 * Math.sin(py * 2.31 - pz * 1.7 + t * 0.5);
  const psiY =
    Math.sin(pz * 1.27 - t * 0.6) * Math.cos(px * 0.97 + t * 0.35) +
    0.45 * Math.cos(pz * 2.11 + px * 1.43 - t * 0.45);
  const psiZ =
    Math.sin(px * 1.19 + t * 0.55) * Math.cos(py * 1.07 - t * 0.65) +
    0.45 * Math.sin(px * 1.91 - py * 2.17 + t * 0.3);
  return [psiX, psiY, psiZ];
}

export interface CurlNoiseSample {
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  vz: number;
  divergence: number;
}

/**
 * Calcula el rotacional 3D (Curl Noise = ∇ × ψ) mediante diferencias centrales
 * garantizando un campo de velocidad incompresible (∇·v ≈ 0).
 */
export function computeCurlNoise3D(
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  t = 0,
  freq = 1.25,
  eps = 1e-4,
): CurlNoiseSample {
  const sampleAt = (sx: number, sy: number, sz: number): [number, number, number] => {
    const inv = 1 / (2 * eps);
    const [, pyP, pzP] = potentialFieldPsi(sx, sy + eps, sz, t, freq);
    const [, pyM, pzM] = potentialFieldPsi(sx, sy - eps, sz, t, freq);
    const dPsiZ_dy = (pzP - pzM) * inv;
    const dPsiY_dy_unused = pyP - pyM;
    void dPsiY_dy_unused;

    const [pxZp, pyZp] = potentialFieldPsi(sx, sy, sz + eps, t, freq);
    const [pxZm, pyZm] = potentialFieldPsi(sx, sy, sz - eps, t, freq);
    const dPsiY_dz = (pyZp - pyZm) * inv;
    const dPsiX_dz = (pxZp - pxZm) * inv;

    const [pxXp, , pzXp] = potentialFieldPsi(sx + eps, sy, sz, t, freq);
    const [pxXm, , pzXm] = potentialFieldPsi(sx - eps, sy, sz, t, freq);
    const dPsiZ_dx = (pzXp - pzXm) * inv;
    const dPsiX_dx_unused = pxXp - pxXm;
    void dPsiX_dx_unused;

    const [pxYp] = potentialFieldPsi(sx, sy + eps, sz, t, freq);
    const [pxYm] = potentialFieldPsi(sx, sy - eps, sz, t, freq);
    const dPsiX_dy = (pxYp - pxYm) * inv;

    const [, pyXp] = potentialFieldPsi(sx + eps, sy, sz, t, freq);
    const [, pyXm] = potentialFieldPsi(sx - eps, sy, sz, t, freq);
    const dPsiY_dx = (pyXp - pyXm) * inv;

    return [
      dPsiZ_dy - dPsiY_dz,
      dPsiX_dz - dPsiZ_dx,
      dPsiY_dx - dPsiX_dy,
    ];
  };

  const [vx, vy, vz] = sampleAt(x, y, z);

  // Verificación numérica de incompresibilidad (∇·v = ∂vx/∂x + ∂vy/∂y + ∂vz/∂z)
  const h = 1e-3;
  const inv2h = 1 / (2 * h);
  const [vxP] = sampleAt(x + h, y, z);
  const [vxM] = sampleAt(x - h, y, z);
  const [, vyP] = sampleAt(x, y + h, z);
  const [, vyM] = sampleAt(x, y - h, z);
  const [, , vzP] = sampleAt(x, y, z + h);
  const [, , vzM] = sampleAt(x, y, z - h);
  const divergence = (vxP - vxM) * inv2h + (vyP - vyM) * inv2h + (vzP - vzM) * inv2h;

  return { vx, vy, vz, divergence };
}

/**
 * Ley Némesis de Cauchy: disipación viscoelástica exponencial
 * Tensión_{t+1} = Tensión_t - Tensión_t * γ hacia el suelo térmico 0.0f.
 */
export function stepNemesisCauchy(tension: number, gamma = 0.08): number {
  const safeGamma = Math.min(0.9999, Math.max(1e-5, gamma));
  const next = tension - tension * safeGamma;
  return Math.abs(next) < 1e-8 ? 0 : next;
}

export interface PauliSoAPhysicsState {
  vx: Float32Array;
  vy: Float32Array;
  vz: Float32Array;
  tension: Float32Array;
}

export function createPauliSoAPhysicsState(capacity: number): PauliSoAPhysicsState {
  const safeCap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(capacity));
  return {
    vx: new Float32Array(safeCap),
    vy: new Float32Array(safeCap),
    vz: new Float32Array(safeCap),
    tension: new Float32Array(safeCap),
  };
}

export interface PauliSoAPhysicsStepReport {
  maxTension: number;
  meanTension: number;
  totalKineticEnergy: number;
  maxCurlDivergence: number;
}

/**
 * Integra un paso físico completo sobre el búfer contiguo Pauli-SoA (28 B / celda):
 * Curl Noise (Navier-Stokes incompresible) + Viscoelasticidad Kelvin-Voigt + Ley Némesis de Cauchy.
 */
export function stepPauliSoAPhysics(
  soa: SoAHolographicBuffer,
  phys: PauliSoAPhysicsState,
  timeSec: number,
  dt = 1 / 120,
  options: {
    curlGain?: number;
    elasticityE?: number;
    viscosityEta?: number;
    nemesisGamma?: number;
  } = {},
): PauliSoAPhysicsStepReport {
  const curlGain = options.curlGain ?? 0.35;
  const elasticityE = options.elasticityE ?? 16.0;
  const viscosityEta = options.viscosityEta ?? 5.5;
  const nemesisGamma = options.nemesisGamma ?? 0.06;

  let maxTension = 0;
  let sumTension = 0;
  let totalKineticEnergy = 0;
  let maxCurlDivergence = 0;

  const n = soa.capacity;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x = soa.x[i];
    const y = soa.y[i];
    const z = soa.z[i];

    const curl =
      curlGain > 0
        ? computeCurlNoise3D(x, y, z, timeSec)
        : { vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, divergence: 0 };
    if (Math.abs(curl.divergence) > maxCurlDivergence) {
      maxCurlDivergence = Math.abs(curl.divergence);
    }

    // Fuerza elástica Kelvin-Voigt + advección Curl Noise
    const accelX = curl.vx * curlGain - elasticityE * phys.tension[i] * 0.02 - viscosityEta * phys.vx[i];
    const accelY = curl.vy * curlGain - elasticityE * phys.tension[i] * 0.02 - viscosityEta * phys.vy[i];
    const accelZ = curl.vz * curlGain - elasticityE * phys.tension[i] * 0.02 - viscosityEta * phys.vz[i];

    phys.vx[i] += accelX * dt;
    phys.vy[i] += accelY * dt;
    phys.vz[i] += accelZ * dt;

    soa.x[i] = x + phys.vx[i] * dt;
    soa.y[i] = y + phys.vy[i] * dt;
    soa.z[i] = z + phys.vz[i] * dt;

    const curlSpeed = Math.hypot(curl.vx, curl.vy, curl.vz) * curlGain;
    const rawTension = phys.tension[i] + curlSpeed * dt;
    const decayedTension = stepNemesisCauchy(rawTension, nemesisGamma);
    phys.tension[i] = decayedTension;

    soa.amp[i] = Math.max(0, soa.amp[i] * (1 - nemesisGamma * 0.5) + decayedTension);

    if (decayedTension > maxTension) maxTension = decayedTension;
    sumTension += decayedTension;
    const vSq = phys.vx[i] * phys.vx[i] + phys.vy[i] * phys.vy[i] + phys.vz[i] * phys.vz[i];
    totalKineticEnergy += 0.5 * vSq;
  }

  return {
    maxTension,
    meanTension: n > 0 ? sumTension / n : 0,
    totalKineticEnergy,
    maxCurlDivergence,
  };
}

/**
 * Optimización de Silicio: Ley Psicofísica de Weber-Fechner para filtrado de fotogramas a 120 FPS.
 */
export function evaluateWeberFechnerFrame(
  prevMetric: number,
  nextMetric: number,
  thresholdK = 0.015,
): { decision: 'RENDER_FRAME' | 'SKIP_FRAME'; deltaRatio: number } {
  const denom = Math.max(1e-4, Math.abs(prevMetric));
  const deltaRatio = Math.abs(nextMetric - prevMetric) / denom;
  return {
    decision: deltaRatio < thresholdK ? 'SKIP_FRAME' : 'RENDER_FRAME',
    deltaRatio,
  };
}

/**
 * Estado cuántico en la Esfera de Bloch: |ψ⟩ = α|0⟩ + β|1⟩ con ||ψ||² = 1.
 */
export interface BlochState {
  alpha: ComplexNumber;
  beta: ComplexNumber;
  theta: number;
  phi: number;
  blochVector: [number, number, number];
  normSq: number;
}

function normalizeSpinor(alpha: ComplexNumber, beta: ComplexNumber): BlochState {
  const norm = Math.sqrt(Math.max(1e-18, cAbsSq(alpha) + cAbsSq(beta)));
  const a = { re: alpha.re / norm, im: alpha.im / norm };
  const b = { re: beta.re / norm, im: beta.im / norm };
  const normSq = cAbsSq(a) + cAbsSq(b);

  // b_x = 2 Re(conj(a)*b), b_y = 2 Im(conj(a)*b), b_z = |a|^2 - |b|^2
  const ab = cMul(cConj(a), b);
  const bx = 2 * ab.re;
  const by = 2 * ab.im;
  const bz = cAbsSq(a) - cAbsSq(b);

  const clampedBz = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, bz));
  const theta = Math.acos(clampedBz);
  const phi = Math.atan2(by, bx);

  return {
    alpha: a,
    beta: b,
    theta,
    phi,
    blochVector: [bx, by, bz],
    normSq,
  };
}

export function createBlochState(theta = 0, phi = 0): BlochState {
  const half = theta / 2;
  const alpha: ComplexNumber = { re: Math.cos(half), im: 0 };
  const s = Math.sin(half);
  const beta: ComplexNumber = { re: s * Math.cos(phi), im: s * Math.sin(phi) };
  return normalizeSpinor(alpha, beta);
}

/**
 * Evolución unitaria exacta de Schrödinger U(t) = exp(-i H t / ħ)
 * bajo un Hamiltoniano de Pauli H = (ħω/2)(nx σx + ny σy + nz σz).
 * Garantiza conservación estricta de probabilidad ||ψ||² = 1.
 */
export function evolveSchrodingerPauli(
  state: BlochState,
  axis: [number, number, number],
  omegaRadPerSec: number,
  dt: number,
): BlochState {
  const axisNorm = Math.hypot(axis[0], axis[1], axis[2]) || 1;
  const nx = axis[0] / axisNorm;
  const ny = axis[1] / axisNorm;
  const nz = axis[2] / axisNorm;

  const halfAngle = 0.5 * omegaRadPerSec * dt;
  const c = Math.cos(halfAngle);
  const s = Math.sin(halfAngle);

  // U = cos(a) I - i sin(a) (nx σx + ny σy + nz σz)
  // U00 = c - i*s*nz, U01 = -i*s*nx - s*ny
  // U10 = -i*s*nx + s*ny, U11 = c + i*s*nz
  const u00: ComplexNumber = { re: c, im: -s * nz };
  const u01: ComplexNumber = { re: -s * ny, im: -s * nx };
  const u10: ComplexNumber = { re: s * ny, im: -s * nx };
  const u11: ComplexNumber = { re: c, im: s * nz };

  const nextAlpha = cAdd(cMul(u00, state.alpha), cMul(u01, state.beta));
  const nextBeta = cAdd(cMul(u10, state.alpha), cMul(u11, state.beta));
  return normalizeSpinor(nextAlpha, nextBeta);
}

/**
 * Proyector ortogonal Hermítico 2×2 sobre C² para el reticulado de Birkhoff-von Neumann.
 */
export interface Projector2x2 {
  m00: ComplexNumber;
  m01: ComplexNumber;
  m10: ComplexNumber;
  m11: ComplexNumber;
  rank: 0 | 1 | 2;
}

const PROJ_ZERO: Projector2x2 = {
  m00: { re: 0, im: 0 },
  m01: { re: 0, im: 0 },
  m10: { re: 0, im: 0 },
  m11: { re: 0, im: 0 },
  rank: 0,
};

const PROJ_IDENTITY: Projector2x2 = {
  m00: { re: 1, im: 0 },
  m01: { re: 0, im: 0 },
  m10: { re: 0, im: 0 },
  m11: { re: 1, im: 0 },
  rank: 2,
};

export function projectorFromBlochAngle(theta: number, phi = 0): Projector2x2 {
  const st = createBlochState(theta, phi);
  return {
    m00: cMul(st.alpha, cConj(st.alpha)),
    m01: cMul(st.alpha, cConj(st.beta)),
    m10: cMul(st.beta, cConj(st.alpha)),
    m11: cMul(st.beta, cConj(st.beta)),
    rank: 1,
  };
}

function projectorDistance(a: Projector2x2, b: Projector2x2): number {
  return Math.sqrt(
    cAbsSq(cSub(a.m00, b.m00)) +
      cAbsSq(cSub(a.m01, b.m01)) +
      cAbsSq(cSub(a.m10, b.m10)) +
      cAbsSq(cSub(a.m11, b.m11)),
  );
}

/** Ortocomplemento en reticulado de Birkhoff-von Neumann: P⊥ = I - P */
export function bvnNot(p: Projector2x2): Projector2x2 {
  if (p.rank === 0) return PROJ_IDENTITY;
  if (p.rank === 2) return PROJ_ZERO;
  return {
    m00: cSub({ re: 1, im: 0 }, p.m00),
    m01: { re: -p.m01.re, im: -p.m01.im },
    m10: { re: -p.m10.re, im: -p.m10.im },
    m11: cSub({ re: 1, im: 0 }, p.m11),
    rank: 1,
  };
}

/** Conjunción cuántica (Meet P ∧ Q = intersección de subespacios en C²) */
export function bvnMeet(p: Projector2x2, q: Projector2x2, tol = 1e-6): Projector2x2 {
  if (p.rank === 0 || q.rank === 0) return PROJ_ZERO;
  if (p.rank === 2) return q;
  if (q.rank === 2) return p;
  return projectorDistance(p, q) < tol ? p : PROJ_ZERO;
}

/** Disyunción cuántica (Join P ∨ Q = (P⊥ ∧ Q⊥)⊥ = span cerrado en C²) */
export function bvnJoin(p: Projector2x2, q: Projector2x2, tol = 1e-6): Projector2x2 {
  if (p.rank === 2 || q.rank === 2) return PROJ_IDENTITY;
  if (p.rank === 0) return q;
  if (q.rank === 0) return p;
  return projectorDistance(p, q) < tol ? p : PROJ_IDENTITY;
}

/**
 * Demuestra formalmente la no-distributividad de la lógica cuántica de Birkhoff-von Neumann:
 * P ∧ (Q ∨ R) vs (P ∧ Q) ∨ (P ∧ R).
 */
export function verifyBirkhoffVonNeumannNonDistributivity(
  p: Projector2x2,
  q: Projector2x2,
  r: Projector2x2,
): {
  distributiveHolds: boolean;
  lhsRank: number;
  rhsRank: number;
  frobeniusDiff: number;
} {
  const lhs = bvnMeet(p, bvnJoin(q, r));
  const rhs = bvnJoin(bvnMeet(p, q), bvnMeet(p, r));
  const frobeniusDiff = projectorDistance(lhs, rhs);
  return {
    distributiveHolds: frobeniusDiff < 1e-6,
    lhsRank: lhs.rank,
    rhsRank: rhs.rank,
    frobeniusDiff,
  };
}


==================== ARCHIVO: C:\HELIX\repo_oficial\src\utils\helixSignal.ts ====================
import { RUNES } from '../data/runes';
import { sha256Hex } from './helixPcc';

export type Z4Symbol = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3;

export interface Qpsk132Frame {
  primeMaskBits: string; // 33 bits representing active P33 primes
  bitLength: 132;
  byteLength: 17;
  payloadBytes: Uint8Array;
  symbols: Z4Symbol[]; // 66 QPSK symbols (2 bits each)
  frameSha256: string;
}

/**
 * Memoria contigua Structure-of-Arrays (SoA) sin punteros ni objetos por celda.
 * 7 arreglos tipados × 4 bytes (Float32) = exactamente 28 bytes contiguos por celda (Zero-GC).
 */
export interface SoAHolographicBuffer {
  readonly capacity: number;
  readonly strideBytes: 28;
  x: Float32Array;
  y: Float32Array;
  z: Float32Array;
  u: Float32Array;
  v: Float32Array;
  amp: Float32Array;
  phaseQuad: Float32Array;
  readonly backingBuffer: ArrayBuffer;
}

export function createSoABuffer(capacity: number): SoAHolographicBuffer {
  const safeCap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(capacity));
  const backingBuffer = new ArrayBuffer(safeCap * 28);
  const stride = safeCap * 4;
  return {
    capacity: safeCap,
    strideBytes: 28,
    x: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 0, safeCap),
    y: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 1, safeCap),
    z: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 2, safeCap),
    u: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 3, safeCap),
    v: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 4, safeCap),
    amp: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 5, safeCap),
    phaseQuad: new Float32Array(backingBuffer, stride * 6, safeCap),
    backingBuffer,
  };
}

/**
 * Codifica hasta 4 canales de 33 bits (4 × 33 = 132 bits = 66 símbolos Z4 QPSK)
 * sobre un arreglo empaquetado de 17 bytes con firma SHA-256 real.
 */
export function encodeQpsk132Frame(
  activePrimeIndices: number[],
  channelValues: [number, number, number] = [0, 0, 0],
): Qpsk132Frame {
  const bits = new Uint8Array(132);

  // Canal 0 (bits 0..32): máscara exacta de los 33 primos P33 activos
  for (const idx of activePrimeIndices) {
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < 33) {
      bits[idx] = 1;
    }
  }

  // Canales 1..3 (bits 33..131): 3 enteros de 33 bits (coordenadas, amplitud, fase)
  for (let ch = 0; ch < 3; ch++) {
    const val = BigInt(Math.max(0, Math.floor(channelValues[ch] ?? 0))) & ((1n << 33n) - 1n);
    for (let b = 0; b < 33; b++) {
      bits[33 * (ch + 1) + b] = Number((val >> BigInt(b)) & 1n);
    }
  }

  const symbols: Z4Symbol[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 132; i += 2) {
    const sym = ((bits[i] << 1) | bits[i + 1]) as Z4Symbol;
    symbols.push(sym);
  }

  const payloadBytes = new Uint8Array(17);
  for (let i = 0; i < 132; i++) {
    if (bits[i] === 1) {
      payloadBytes[i >> 3] |= 1 << (i & 7);
    }
  }

  const hexFrame = Array.from(payloadBytes)
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('');

  return {
    primeMaskBits: Array.from(bits.slice(0, 33)).join(''),
    bitLength: 132,
    byteLength: 17,
    payloadBytes,
    symbols,
    frameSha256: sha256Hex(`qpsk132|${hexFrame}`),
  };
}

export function decodeQpsk132Symbols(symbols: Z4Symbol[]): {
  activePrimeIndices: number[];
  channels: [number, number, number];
} {
  const bits = new Uint8Array(132);
  for (let s = 0; s < 66 && s < symbols.length; s++) {
    const sym = symbols[s] & 3;
    bits[s * 2] = (sym >> 1) & 1;
    bits[s * 2 + 1] = sym & 1;
  }

  const activePrimeIndices: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 33; i++) {
    if (bits[i] === 1) activePrimeIndices.push(i);
  }

  const channels: [number, number, number] = [0, 0, 0];
  for (let ch = 0; ch < 3; ch++) {
    let val = 0n;
    for (let b = 0; b < 33; b++) {
      if (bits[33 * (ch + 1) + b] === 1) {
        val |= 1n << BigInt(b);
      }
    }
    channels[ch] = Number(val);
  }

  return { activePrimeIndices, channels };
}

/**
 * Empaqueta símbolos QPSK y parámetros espectrales en la memoria contigua SoA de 28 bytes/celda.
 */
export function packToSoABuffer(
  buffer: SoAHolographicBuffer,
  slot: number,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  u: number,
  v: number,
  amp: number,
  phaseQuad: Z4Symbol,
): void {
  if (slot < 0 || slot >= buffer.capacity) return;
  buffer.x[slot] = x;
  buffer.y[slot] = y;
  buffer.z[slot] = z;
  buffer.u[slot] = u;
  buffer.v[slot] = v;
  buffer.amp[slot] = amp;
  buffer.phaseQuad[slot] = phaseQuad;
}

/**
 * Purga explícita de memoria SoA con entropía CSPRNG (Zeroization real).
 */
export function zeroizeSoABuffer(buffer: SoAHolographicBuffer): void {
  const view = new Uint8Array(buffer.backingBuffer);
  if (typeof globalThis.crypto !== 'undefined' && globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues) {
    globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(view);
  } else {
    view.fill(0);
  }
}

/**
 * Ley de dispersión óptica de Cauchy: n(lambda) = A + B/lambda^2 + C/lambda^4 (lambda en micrómetros).
 */
export function cauchyRefractiveIndex(wavelengthNm: number, A = 1.458, B = 0.00354, C = 0.00012): number {
  const lambdaUm = Math.max(0.35, wavelengthNm / 1000);
  const l2 = lambdaUm * lambdaUm;
  return Number((A + B / l2 + C / (l2 * l2)).toFixed(6));
}

/**
 * Filtro perceptual Weber-Fechner: devuelve true si el cambio relativo ΔI/I supera el umbral k.
 */
export function passesWeberFechnerThreshold(prevIntensity: number, nextIntensity: number, k = 0.02): boolean {
  const base = Math.max(1e-4, Math.abs(prevIntensity));
  return Math.abs(nextIntensity - prevIntensity) / base >= k;
}

/**
 * Paso discreto de viscoelasticidad Kelvin-Voigt: sigma = E*epsilon + eta*(dEpsilon/dt).
 */
export function stepKelvinVoigt(
  strain: number,
  velocity: number,
  targetStrain: number,
  dt: number,
  elasticityE = 18.0,
  viscosityEta = 6.5,
): { nextStrain: number; nextVelocity: number; stress: number } {
  const error = targetStrain - strain;
  const accel = elasticityE * error - viscosityEta * velocity;
  const nextVelocity = velocity + accel * dt;
  const nextStrain = strain + nextVelocity * dt;
  const stress = elasticityE * nextStrain + viscosityEta * nextVelocity;
  return { nextStrain, nextVelocity, stress };
}

export function extractActiveRunesFromScript(glyphsList: string[]): number[] {
  const byGlyph = new Map(RUNES.map((r) => [r.glyph, r.index]));
  const indices = new Set<number>();
  for (const gStr of glyphsList) {
    for (const ch of Array.from(gStr)) {
      const idx = byGlyph.get(ch);
      if (idx !== undefined) indices.add(idx);
    }
  }
  return Array.from(indices).sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

const DEFAULT_DOMINANT_RUNE: PrimitiveToken = TOKEN_BY_GLYPH.get('ᚩ') ?? RUNES[3];

function extractRunesFromCompiled(glyphLines: string[]): PrimitiveToken[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: PrimitiveToken[] = [];
  for (const line of glyphLines) {
    for (const ch of line) {
      const rune = TOKEN_BY_GLYPH.get(ch);
      if (rune && !seen.has(rune.glyph)) {
        seen.add(rune.glyph);
        out.push(rune);
      }
    }
  }
  if (out.length === 0) {
    for (const g of ['ᛇ', 'ᚢ', 'ᚩ', 'ᚳ']) {
      const r = TOKEN_BY_GLYPH.get(g);
      if (r) out.push(r);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function createSiliconBridgeState(capacity = 32): TokenPauliBridgeState {
  const cap = Math.max(4, Math.min(256, Math.round(capacity)));
  const cauchyTriple = computeCauchySpectralTriple();
  const state: TokenPauliBridgeState = {
    soaBuffer: createSoABuffer(cap),
    phys: createPauliSoAPhysicsState(cap),
    bloch: createBlochState(Math.PI / 4, 0),
    cauchyTriple,
    activeRunes: [DEFAULT_DOMINANT_RUNE],
    dominantRune: DEFAULT_DOMINANT_RUNE,
    cymaticHz: DEFAULT_DOMINANT_RUNE.frequencyHz,
    curlGain: 0.45,
    nemesisGamma: 0.055,
    weberThresholdK: 0.015,
    impulseEnvelope: 0,
    lastTension: 0,
    lastMetric: 0,
    timeSec: 0,
    renderedFrames: 0,
    skippedFrames: 0,
    lastWeberDecision: 'RENDER_FRAME',
    smtVerified: true,
    smtLatencyMs: 0,
    lastRejectedReason: null,
    lastImpulseLabel: 'Inicializado en reposo (0.0f)',
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < cap; i++) {
    const angle = i * 2.399963229728653;
    const rad = 0.25 + (i / cap) * 0.75;
    packToSoABuffer(
      state.soaBuffer,
      i,
      Math.cos(angle) * rad,
      (i / cap - 0.5) * 0.6,
      Math.sin(angle) * rad,
      0,
      0,
      0.35,
      (i % 4) as Z4Symbol,
    );
  }
  return state;
}

/**
 * Traduce un script Helix validado directamente a memoria Pauli-SoA (28 B/celda, Zero-GC).
 * Blindaje SMT Inviolable: si verifyHelixProgram devuelve RECHAZADO, no modifica ni SoA ni GPU.
 */
export function syncScriptToSiliconBridge(
  state: TokenPauliBridgeState,
  script: string,
  vocabulary?: CompoundWord[],
): {
  accepted: boolean;
  verification: ProgramVerificationReport;
  activeRunesCount: number;
} {
  const instructions = compileHelixScript(script, vocabulary);
  const verification = verifyHelixProgram(instructions);
  const smtLatencyMs = verification.symbolic?.smtLatencyMs ?? 0;
  state.smtLatencyMs = smtLatencyMs;

  if (verification.status === 'RECHAZADO') {
    state.smtVerified = false;
    const firstErr = verification.findings.find((f) => f.severity === 'ERROR');
    state.lastRejectedReason = firstErr
      ? `L${firstErr.lineNumber} [${firstErr.rule}]: ${firstErr.detail}`
      : 'Violación formal detectada por Z3 SMT';
    return {
      accepted: false,
      verification,
      activeRunesCount: state.activeRunes.length,
    };
  }

  let requestedCap = state.soaBuffer.capacity;
  let nextCurlGain = 0.45;
  let nextNemesisGamma = 0.055;
  let nextWeberK = 0.015;
  let blochAxis: [number, number, number] = [0, 1, 0];
  let blochOmega = 1.1;

  for (const inst of instructions) {
    if (inst.opcode === 'SOA_INIT') {
      const cap = Math.round(Number(inst.args[0]));
      if (Number.isFinite(cap) && cap >= 4 && cap <= 256) {
        requestedCap = cap;
      }
    } else if (inst.opcode === 'CURL_STEP') {
      const g = Number(inst.args[0]);
      if (Number.isFinite(g) && g > 0 && g <= 4) {
        nextCurlGain = g;
      }
    } else if (inst.opcode === 'NEMESIS_DECAY') {
      const g = Number(inst.args[0]);
      if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 0.005 && g < 1) {
        nextNemesisGamma = g;
      }
    } else if (inst.opcode === 'WEBER_GUARD') {
      const k = Number(inst.args[0]);
      if (Number.isFinite(k) && k > 0 && k < 0.5) {
        nextWeberK = k;
      }
    } else if (inst.opcode === 'BLOCH_ROT') {
      const nx = Number(inst.args[0] ?? 0);
      const ny = Number(inst.args[1] ?? 1);
      const nz = Number(inst.args[2] ?? 0);
      const om = Number(inst.args[3] ?? 1.1);
      if (Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) > 0 && Number.isFinite(om)) {
        blochAxis = [nx, ny, nz];
        blochOmega = om;
      }
    }
  }

  if (requestedCap !== state.soaBuffer.capacity) {
    state.soaBuffer = createSoABuffer(requestedCap);
    state.phys = createPauliSoAPhysicsState(requestedCap);
  }

  const activeRunes = extractRunesFromCompiled(instructions.map((i) => i.glyphs));
  const preferred =
    activeRunes.find((r) => r.glyph === 'ᚩ') ?? activeRunes[activeRunes.length - 1] ?? DEFAULT_DOMINANT_RUNE;
  const meanHz =
    activeRunes.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.frequencyHz, 0) / Math.max(1, activeRunes.length);

  state.activeRunes = activeRunes;
  state.dominantRune = preferred;
  state.cymaticHz = Number(meanHz.toFixed(4));
  state.curlGain = nextCurlGain;
  state.nemesisGamma = nextNemesisGamma;
  state.weberThresholdK = nextWeberK;
  state.smtVerified = true;
  state.lastRejectedReason = null;

  const cap = state.soaBuffer.capacity;
  for (let i = 0; i < cap; i++) {
    const rune = activeRunes[i % activeRunes.length];
    const angle = i * 2.399963229728653 + (rune.prime % 11) * 0.25;
    const rad = 0.25 + (i / cap) * 0.75;
    const amp = rune.frequencyHz / 520;
    packToSoABuffer(
      state.soaBuffer,
      i,
      Math.cos(angle) * rad,
      (i / cap - 0.5) * 0.6,
      Math.sin(angle) * rad,
      0,
      0,
      amp,
      (rune.prime % 4) as Z4Symbol,
    );
    state.phys.tension[i] = 0.35 + (rune.prime / 137) * 0.45;
    state.phys.vx[i] = Math.cos(angle) * 0.14;
